Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa

Homage to that Blessed One, the Worthy One, the Perfectly Enlightened One.

Abhidhammapiṭake

In the Abhidhamma Piṭaka

Dhātukathāpāḷi

The Discourse on Elements

Uddeso

The Heading

1. Nayamātikā

1. The Methodical Matrix

1. (1) Saṅgaho [T 0.0001][P 0.0001][M 0.0001][V 0.0001] asaṅgaho (2) saṅgahitena asaṅgahitaṃ (3) asaṅgahitena saṅgahitaṃ (4) saṅgahitena saṅgahitaṃ (5) asaṅgahitena asaṅgahitaṃ (6) sampayogo vippayogo (7)sampayuttena vippayuttaṃ (8) vippayuttena sampayuttaṃ (9) sampayuttena sampayuttaṃ (10)vippayuttena vippayuttaṃ (11) saṅgahitena sampayuttaṃ vippayuttaṃ (12) sampayuttena saṅgahitaṃ asaṅgahitaṃ (13) asaṅgahitena sampayuttaṃ vippayuttaṃ (14) vippayuttena saṅgahitaṃ asaṅgahitaṃ.

1. (1) Inclusion, non-inclusion. (2) By what is included, what is not included. (3) By what is not included, what is included. (4) By what is included, what is included. (5) By what is not included, what is not included. (6) Association, dissociation. (7) By what is associated, what is dissociated. (8) By what is dissociated, what is associated. (9) By what is associated, what is associated. (10) By what is dissociated, what is dissociated. (11) By what is included, what is associated and what is dissociated. (12) By what is associated, what is included and what is not included. (13) By what is not included, what is associated and what is dissociated. (14) By what is dissociated, what is included and what is not included.

2. Abbhantaramātikā

2. The Internal Matrix

2. (1) Pañcakkhandhā [V 0.0002] (2) dvādasāyatanāni (3) aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo (4) cattāri saccāni (5) bāvīsatindriyāni (6) paṭiccasamuppādo (7) cattāro satipaṭṭhānā (8)cattāro sammappadhānā (9) cattāro iddhipādā (10) cattāri jhānāni (11) catasso appamaññāyo (12) pañcindriyāni (13) pañca balāni (14) satta bojjhaṅgā (15)ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo (16) phasso vedanā saññā cetanā cittaṃ adhimokkho manasikāro.

2. (1) The five aggregates, (2) the twelve sense bases, (3) the eighteen elements, (4) the four truths, (5) the twenty-two faculties, (6) dependent origination, (7) the four foundations of mindfulness, (8) the four right exertions, (9) the four bases of spiritual power, (10) the four jhānas, (11) the four immeasurables, (12) the five faculties, (13) the five powers, (14) the seven factors of enlightenment, (15) the Noble Eight-factored Path, (16) contact, feeling, perception, volition, consciousness, decision, attention.

3. Nayamukhamātikā

3. The Matrix of the Method's Entrance

3. Tīhi [M 0.0002] saṅgaho, tīhi asaṅgaho, catūhi sampayogo, catūhi vippayogo.

3. Inclusion by three, non-inclusion by three, association with four, dissociation from four.

4. Lakkhaṇamātikā

4. The Characteristic Matrix

4. Sabhāgo, visabhāgo.

4. Compatible, incompatible.

5. Bāhiramātikā

5. The External Matrix

5. Sabbāpi dhammasaṅgaṇī dhātukathāya mātikāti.

5. The entire mātikā of the Dhammasaṅgaṇī is the mātikā for the Dhātukathā.

1. Paṭhamanayo

1. The First Method

1. Saṅgahāsaṅgahapadaniddeso

1. Exposition of the Terms of Inclusion and Non-Inclusion

1. Khandho

1. Aggregate

6. Rūpakkhandho [T 0.0002][M 0.0003][V 0.0003] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahito? Rūpakkhandho [P 0.0002] ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

6. By how many aggregates, by how many sense bases, and by how many elements is the form aggregate included? The form aggregate is included by one aggregate, by eleven sense bases, and by eleven elements. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, by one sense base, and by seven elements.

7. Vedanākkhandho katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahito? Vedanākkhandho ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

7. By how many aggregates, by how many sense bases, and by how many elements is the feeling aggregate included? The feeling aggregate is included by one aggregate, by one sense base, and by one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by seventeen elements.

8. Saññākkhandho katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahito? Saññākkhandho ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

8. By how many aggregates, by how many sense bases, and by how many elements is the perception aggregate included? The perception aggregate is included by one aggregate, by one sense base, and by one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by seventeen elements.

9. Saṅkhārakkhandho katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahito? Saṅkhārakkhandho ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

9. By how many aggregates, by how many sense bases, and by how many elements is the formations aggregate included? The formations aggregate is included by one aggregate, by one sense base, and by one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by seventeen elements.

10. Viññāṇakkhandho [V 0.0004] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahito? Viññāṇakkhandho ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi [T 0.0003] ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

10. By how many aggregates, by how many sense bases, and by how many elements is the consciousness aggregate included? The consciousness aggregate is included by one aggregate, by one sense base, and by seven elements. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by eleven elements.

(Ekamūlakaṃ.)

(Section of Single Terms)

11. Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi [M 0.0004] ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0003] asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

11. By how many aggregates, by how many sense bases, and by how many elements are the form aggregate and the feeling aggregate included? The form aggregate and the feeling aggregate are included by two aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, by one sense base, and by seven elements.

12. Rūpakkhandho ca saññākkhandho ca…pe… dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

12. The form aggregate and the perception aggregate... are included by two aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, by one sense base, and by seven elements.

13. Rūpakkhandho ca saṅkhārakkhandho ca…pe… dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

13. The form aggregate and the formations aggregate... are included by two aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, by one sense base, and by seven elements.

14. Rūpakkhandho ca viññāṇakkhandho ca…pe… dvīhi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi, na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

14. The form aggregate and the consciousness aggregate... are included by two aggregates, by twelve sense bases, and by eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates; there are no sense bases by which they are not included, nor any elements by which they are not included.

(Dukamūlakaṃ.)

(Section of Double Terms)

15. Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca saññākkhandho ca katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca saññākkhandho ca tīhi [V 0.0005] khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

15. By how many aggregates, by how many sense bases, and by how many elements are the form aggregate, the feeling aggregate, and the perception aggregate included? The form aggregate, the feeling aggregate, and the perception aggregate are included by three aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, by one sense base, and by seven elements.

16. Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca saṅkhārakkhandho ca…pe… tīhi [T 0.0004] khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

16. The form aggregate, the feeling aggregate, and the formations aggregate... are included by three aggregates, by eleven sense bases, and by eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, by one sense base, and by seven elements.

17. Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca viññāṇakkhandho ca…pe… tīhi [P 0.0004] khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi, na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

17. The form aggregate, the feeling aggregate, and the consciousness aggregate... are included by three aggregates, by twelve sense bases, and by eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates; there are no sense bases by which they are not included, nor any elements by which they are not included.

(Tikamūlakaṃ.)

(The section on triads.)

18. Rūpakkhandho [M 0.0005] ca vedanākkhandho ca saññākkhandho ca saṅkhārakkhandho ca katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca saññākkhandho ca saṅkhārakkhandho ca catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

18. The aggregate of form, and the aggregate of feeling, and the aggregate of perception, and the aggregate of formations: by how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are they included? The aggregate of form, and the aggregate of feeling, and the aggregate of perception, and the aggregate of formations are included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements.

19. Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca saññākkhandho ca viññāṇakkhandho ca…pe… catūhi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena, na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

19. The aggregate of form, and the aggregate of feeling, and the aggregate of perception, and the aggregate of consciousness... are included by four aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate; there are no bases by which they are not included, nor any elements by which they are not included.

(Catukkamūlakaṃ.)

(The section on quartets.)

20. Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca saññākkhandho ca saṅkhārakkhandho ca viññāṇakkhandho ca katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Rūpakkhandho ca vedanākkhandho ca saññākkhandho ca saṅkhārakkhandho ca viññāṇakkhandho ca pañcahi khandhehi dvādasāyatanehi [T 0.0005][V 0.0006] aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

20. The aggregate of form, and the aggregate of feeling, and the aggregate of perception, and the aggregate of formations, and the aggregate of consciousness: by how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are they included? The aggregate of form, and the aggregate of feeling, and the aggregate of perception, and the aggregate of formations, and the aggregate of consciousness are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates by which they are not included, nor any bases by which they are not included, nor any elements by which they are not included.

21. Pañcakkhandhā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Pañcakkhandhā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

21. The five aggregates: by how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are they included? The five aggregates are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates by which they are not included, nor any bases by which they are not included, nor any elements by which they are not included.

(Pañcakaṃ.)

(The Pentad.)

2. Āyatanaṃ

2. Bases

22. Cakkhāyatanaṃ [P 0.0005] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ? Cakkhāyatanaṃ ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

22. The eye-base: by how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements is it included? The eye-base is included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

23. Sotāyatanaṃ [M 0.0006]… ghānāyatanaṃ… jivhāyatanaṃ… kāyāyatanaṃ… rūpāyatanaṃ… saddāyatanaṃ… gandhāyatanaṃ… rasāyatanaṃ… phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ…pe… ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

23. The ear-base… the nose-base… the tongue-base… the body-base… the form-base… the sound-base… the smell-base… the taste-base… the tangible-base… is included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

24. Manāyatanaṃ ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

24. The mind-base is included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements.

25. Dhammāyatanaṃ asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

25. The dhamma-base—setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates—is included by four aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by one aggregate, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

(Ekamūlakaṃ.)

(The section on singles.)

26. Cakkhāyatanañca [T 0.0006][V 0.0007] sotāyatanañca ekena khandhena dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

26. The eye-base and the ear-base are included by one aggregate, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

27. Cakkhāyatanañca [P 0.0006] ghānāyatanañca… cakkhāyatanañca jivhāyatanañca… cakkhāyatanañca kāyāyatanañca… cakkhāyatanañca rūpāyatanañca… cakkhāyatanañca saddāyatanañca… cakkhāyatanañca gandhāyatanañca… cakkhāyatanañca rasāyatanañca… cakkhāyatanañca phoṭṭhabbāyatanañca ekena khandhena dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

27. The eye-base and the nose-base... the eye-base and the tongue-base... the eye-base and the body-base... the eye-base and the form-base... the eye-base and the sound-base... the eye-base and the smell-base... the eye-base and the taste-base... the eye-base and the tangible-base are included by one aggregate, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

28. Cakkhāyatanañca manāyatanañca dvīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

28. The eye-base and the mind-base are included by two aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, ten bases, and ten elements.

29. Cakkhāyatanañca dhammāyatanañca asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

29. The eye-base and the dhamma-base—setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates—are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

(Dukamūlakaṃ.)

(The section on pairs.)

30. Dvādasāyatanāni [M 0.0007] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Dvādasāyatanāni asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

30. By how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are the twelve bases included? The twelve bases—setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates—are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates by which they are not included, nor any bases by which they are not included, nor any elements by which they are not included.

(Dvādasakaṃ.)

(The group of twelve.)

3. Dhātu

3. Elements

31. Cakkhudhātu [T 0.0007][V 0.0008] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Cakkhudhātu ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi [P 0.0007] khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

31. The eye-element: by how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements is it included? The eye-element is included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

32. Sotadhātu… ghānadhātu… jivhādhātu… kāyadhātu… rūpadhātu… saddadhātu… gandhadhātu… rasadhātu… phoṭṭhabbadhātu… cakkhuviññāṇadhātu… sotaviññāṇadhātu… ghānaviññāṇadhātu… jivhāviññāṇadhātu… kāyaviññāṇadhātu… manodhātu… manoviññāṇadhātu ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

32. The ear-element… the nose-element… the tongue-element… the body-element… the form-element… the sound-element… the smell-element… the taste-element… the tangible-element… the eye-consciousness-element… the ear-consciousness-element… the nose-consciousness-element… the tongue-consciousness-element… the body-consciousness-element… the mind-element… the mind-consciousness-element is included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

33. Dhammadhātu asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

33. The dhamma-element, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregates, is included by four aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by one aggregate, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

(Ekamūlakaṃ.)

(Based on One.)

34. Cakkhudhātu ca sotadhātu ca ekena khandhena dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

34. The eye-element and the ear-element are included by one aggregate, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

35. Cakkhudhātu ca ghānadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca jivhādhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca kāyadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca rūpadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca saddadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu [M 0.0008] ca gandhadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca rasadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca phoṭṭhabbadhātu ca ekena [P 0.0008] khandhena dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi [T 0.0008] dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

35. The eye-element and the nose-element... the eye-element and the tongue-element... the eye-element and the body-element... the eye-element and the form-element... the eye-element and the sound-element... the eye-element and the smell-element... the eye-element and the taste-element... the eye-element and the tangible-element are included by one aggregate, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

36. Cakkhudhātu [V 0.0009] ca cakkhuviññāṇadhātu ca dvīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

36. The eye-element and the eye-consciousness-element are included by two aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

37. Cakkhudhātu ca sotaviññāṇadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca ghānaviññāṇadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca jivhāviññāṇadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca kāyaviññāṇadhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca manodhātu ca… cakkhudhātu ca manoviññāṇadhātu ca dvīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

37. The eye-element and the ear-consciousness-element… the eye-element and the nose-consciousness-element… the eye-element and the tongue-consciousness-element… the eye-element and the body-consciousness-element… the eye-element and the mind-element… the eye-element and the mind-consciousness-element are included by two aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

38. Cakkhudhātu ca dhammadhātu ca asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

38. The eye-element and the dhamma-element, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregates, are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

(Dukamūlakaṃ.)

(Rooted in Dyads.)

39. Aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Aṭṭhārasa dhātuyo asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

39. By how many aggregates, bases, and elements are the eighteen elements included? The eighteen elements, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which they are not included.

(Aṭṭhārasakaṃ.)

(The Group of Eighteen.)

4. Saccaṃ

4. The Truths

40. Dukkhasaccaṃ [T 0.0009] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ? Dukkhasaccaṃ [P 0.0009] pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

40. By how many aggregates, bases, and elements is the Truth of Suffering included? The Truth of Suffering is included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many is it not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which it is not included.

41. Samudayasaccaṃ [M 0.0009]… [V 0.0010] maggasaccaṃ ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

41. The Truth of Origin and the Truth of the Path are each included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

42. Nirodhasaccaṃ na kehici khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Pañcahi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

42. The Truth of Cessation is not included by any aggregates; it is included by one base and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by five aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

(Ekamūlakaṃ.)

(Section on Singles)

43. Dukkhasaccañca samudayasaccañca pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

43. The Truth of Suffering and the Truth of Origin are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which they are not included.

44. Dukkhasaccañca maggasaccañca pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

44. The Truth of Suffering and the Truth of the Path are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which they are not included.

45. Dukkhasaccañca nirodhasaccañca asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

45. The Truth of Suffering and the Truth of Cessation—setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates—are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which they are not included.

(Dukamūlakaṃ.)

(Section on Pairs)

46. Dukkhasaccañca [T 0.0010] samudayasaccañca maggasaccañca pañcahi [P 0.0010] khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

46. The Truth of Suffering, the Truth of Origin, and the Truth of the Path are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which they are not included.

47. Dukkhasaccañca samudayasaccañca nirodhasaccañca asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi [V 0.0011] dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

47. The Truth of Suffering, the Truth of Origin, and the Truth of Cessation—setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates—are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which they are not included.

(Tikamūlakaṃ.)

(Section on Triads)

48. Dukkhasaccañca [M 0.0010] samudayasaccañca maggasaccañca nirodhasaccañca asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

48. The Truth of Suffering, the Truth of Origin, the Truth of the Path, and the Truth of Cessation—setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates—are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which they are not included.

49. Cattāri saccāni katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitāni? Cattāri saccāni asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitāni. Katihi asaṅgahitāni? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitāni.

49. By how many aggregates, bases, and elements are the Four Truths included? The Four Truths—setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates—are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? There are no aggregates, no bases, and no elements by which they are not included.

(Catukkaṃ.)

(The Tetrad)

5. Indriyaṃ

5. The Faculties

50. Cakkhundriyaṃ katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ? Cakkhundriyaṃ ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi [T 0.0011] sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

50. By how many aggregates, bases, and elements is the eye faculty included? The eye faculty is included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

51. Sotindriyaṃ… ghānindriyaṃ… jivhindriyaṃ… kāyindriyaṃ… itthindriyaṃ [P 0.0011]… purisindriyaṃ ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

51. The ear faculty… the nose faculty… the tongue faculty… the body faculty… the female faculty… the male faculty are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

52. Manindriyaṃ [V 0.0012] ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

52. The mind faculty is included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements.

53. Jīvitindriyaṃ dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Tīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

53. The life faculty is included by two aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by three aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

54. Sukhindriyaṃ [M 0.0011]… dukkhindriyaṃ… somanassindriyaṃ… domanassindriyaṃ… upekkhindriyaṃ… saddhindriyaṃ… vīriyindriyaṃ… satindriyaṃ… samādhindriyaṃ… paññindriyaṃ… anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ… aññindriyaṃ… aññātāvindriyaṃ ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

54. The pleasure faculty… the pain faculty… the joy faculty… the displeasure faculty… the equanimity faculty… the faith faculty… the energy faculty… the mindfulness faculty… the concentration faculty… the wisdom faculty… the faculty of 'I shall know the as-yet-unknown'… the faculty of final knowledge… the faculty of one who has final knowledge are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

(Ekamūlakaṃ.)

(The Section on Singles.)

55. Cakkhundriyañca sotindriyañca ekena [P 0.0012] khandhena dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

55. The eye faculty and the ear faculty are included by one aggregate, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

56. Cakkhundriyañca [T 0.0012] ghānindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca jivhindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca kāyindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca itthindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca purisindriyañca ekena khandhena dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

56. The eye faculty and the nose faculty… the eye faculty and the tongue faculty… the eye faculty and the body faculty… the eye faculty and the female faculty… the eye faculty and the male faculty are included by one aggregate, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

57. Cakkhundriyañca manindriyañca dvīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

57. The eye faculty and the mind faculty are included by two aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, ten bases, and ten elements.

58. Cakkhundriyañca jīvitindriyañca dvīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

58. The eye faculty and the life faculty are included by two aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

59. Cakkhundriyañca [V 0.0013] sukhindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca dukkhindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca somanassindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca domanassindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca upekkhindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca [P 0.0013] saddhindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca vīriyindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca satindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca samādhindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca paññindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca anaññātaññassāmītindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca aññindriyañca… cakkhundriyañca aññātāvindriyañca dvīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

59. The eye faculty and the pleasure faculty… the eye faculty and the pain faculty… the eye faculty and the joy faculty… the eye faculty and the displeasure faculty… the eye faculty and the equanimity faculty… the eye faculty and the faith faculty… the eye faculty and the energy faculty… the eye faculty and the mindfulness faculty… the eye faculty and the concentration faculty… the eye faculty and the wisdom faculty… the eye faculty and the faculty of 'I shall know the as-yet-unknown'… the eye faculty and the faculty of final knowledge… the eye faculty and the faculty of one who has final knowledge—are included by two aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

(Dukamūlakaṃ.)

(The Section of Pairs.)

60. Bāvīsatindriyāni [T 0.0013][M 0.0012] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitāni? Bāvīsatindriyāni catūhi khandhehi sattahāyatanehi terasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitāni. Katihi asaṅgahitāni? Ekena khandhena pañcahāyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitāni.

60. The twenty-two faculties—by how many aggregates, bases, and elements are they included? The twenty-two faculties are included by four aggregates, seven bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, five bases, and five elements.

6. Paṭiccasamuppādādi

6. Dependent Origination, and so forth.

61. Avijjā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

61. Ignorance is included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

62. Avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

62. Volitional formations conditioned by ignorance are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

63. Saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ ekena [P 0.0014] khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

63. Consciousness conditioned by volitional formations is included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements.

64. Viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpaṃ catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

64. Name-and-form conditioned by consciousness is included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements. By how many is it not included? It is not included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements.

65. Nāmarūpapaccayā [V 0.0014] saḷāyatanaṃ dvīhi khandhehi chahāyatanehi dvādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Tīhi khandhehi chahāyatanehi chahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

65. The six bases conditioned by name-and-form are included by two aggregates, six bases, and twelve elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, six bases, and six elements.

66. Saḷāyatanapaccayā phasso… phassapaccayā vedanā… vedanāpaccayā taṇhā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ… kammabhavo [T 0.0014] upādānapaccayā kammabhavo (sī. syā.) ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

66. Contact conditioned by the six bases, feeling conditioned by contact, craving conditioned by feeling, clinging conditioned by craving, and kamma-existence conditioned by clinging are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

67. Upapattibhavo [M 0.0013]… kāmabhavo… saññābhavo… pañcavokārabhavo pañcahi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Na kehici khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahito.

67. Rebirth-existence, desire-realm existence, perception-existence, and the five-aggregate existence are included by five aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates; they are not included by one base and one element.

68. Rūpabhavo pañcahi khandhehi pañcahāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahito. Katihi [P 0.0015] asaṅgahito? Na kehici khandhehi sattahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

68. Form-realm existence is included by five aggregates, five bases, and eight elements. By how many is it not included? It is not un-included by any aggregates; it is not included by seven bases and ten elements.

69. Arūpabhavo… nevasaññānāsaññābhavo… catuvokārabhavo catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

69. Formless-existence, existence of neither-perception-nor-non-perception, and the four-constituent existence are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

70. Asaññābhavo… ekavokārabhavo ekena khandhena dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Catūhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

70. Non-percipient existence and the single-constituent existence are included by one aggregate, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

71. Jāti dvīhi khandhehi… jarā dvīhi khandhehi… maraṇaṃ dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Tīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

71. Birth, aging, and death are included by two aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

72. Soko… paridevo… dukkhaṃ… domanassaṃ… upāyāso… satipaṭṭhānaṃ… sammappadhānaṃ [T 0.0015] ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

72. Sorrow, lamentation, pain, mental pain, distress, the foundations of mindfulness, and right exertion are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

73. Iddhipādo [V 0.0015] dvīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Tīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

73. The bases of psychic power are included by two aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

74. Jhānaṃ [P 0.0016] dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Tīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

74. Jhāna is included by two aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many is it not included? It is not included by three aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

75. Appamaññā [M 0.0014]… pañcindriyāni… pañca balāni… satta bojjhaṅgā… ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo… phasso… vedanā… saññā… cetanā… adhimokkho… manasikāro ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahito. Katihi asaṅgahito? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahito.

75. The immeasurables, the five faculties, the five powers, the seven factors of enlightenment, the noble eightfold path, contact, feeling, perception, volition, determination, and attention are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

76. Cittaṃ ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitaṃ. Katihi asaṅgahitaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitaṃ.

76. Mind is included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements. By how many is it not included? It is not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements.

7. Tikaṃ

7. The Triads

77. Kusalā dhammā… akusalā dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Kusalā dhammā… akusalā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena [T 0.0016] khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

77. By how many aggregates, by how many bases, and by how many elements are wholesome states and unwholesome states included? Wholesome states and unwholesome states are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

78. Abyākatā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

78. Indeterminate states, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, by any bases, or by any elements.

79. Sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā… dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi [P 0.0017] khandhehi dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

79. States associated with pleasant feeling and states associated with painful feeling are included by three aggregates, two bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and fifteen elements.

80. Adukkhamasukhāya [V 0.0016] vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

80. States associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling are included by three aggregates, two bases, and seven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and eleven elements.

81. Vipākā [M 0.0015] dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

81. Resultant states are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

82. Vipākadhammadhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

82. States that produce resultants and states that are defiled and associated with defilements are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

83. Nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi terasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā[T 0.0017]? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

83. States that are neither resultants nor producers of resultants, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates and not un-included by any bases; they are not included by five elements.

84. Upādinnupādāniyā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

84. States that are taken up by kamma and are fuel for clinging are included by five aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates; they are not included by one base and one element.

85. Anupādinnupādāniyā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi sattahāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0018] asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi pañcahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

85. States that are not taken up by kamma but are fuel for clinging are included by five aggregates, seven bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates; they are not included by five bases and ten elements.

86. Anupādinnaanupādāniyā dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

86. States that are neither taken up by kamma nor are fuel for clinging, and states that are undefiled and not associated with defilements, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

87. Asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi [V 0.0017] saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

87. States that are not defiled but are associated with defilements are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, by any bases, or by any elements.

88. Savitakkasavicārā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

88. States with applied thought and sustained thought are included in four aggregates, two bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements.

89. Avitakkavicāramattā [M 0.0016] dhammā… pītisahagatā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena [T 0.0018] khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

89. States without applied thought, with only sustained thought, and states accompanied by rapture are included in four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

90. Avitakkaavicārā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

90. States without applied thought and without sustained thought, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases; they are un-included by one element.

91. Sukhasahagatā dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

91. States accompanied by pleasure are included in three aggregates, two bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in two aggregates, ten bases, and fifteen elements.

92. Upekkhāsahagatā [P 0.0019] dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

92. States accompanied by equanimity are included in three aggregates, two bases, and seven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in two aggregates, ten bases, and eleven elements.

93. Dassanena pahātabbā dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā… ācayagāmino dhammā… apacayagāmino dhammā… sekkhā dhammā… asekkhā dhammā… mahaggatā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

93. States to be abandoned by seeing, states to be abandoned by development, states with causes to be abandoned by seeing, states with causes to be abandoned by development, states leading to accumulation, states leading to dispersal, states of a learner, states of one beyond training, and sublime states are included in four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

94. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā… neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā… nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino dhammā… nevasekkhanāsekkhā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ [V 0.0018] khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi [T 0.0019] dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

94. States to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development, states with causes to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development, states leading neither to accumulation nor to dispersal, and states of neither a learner nor one beyond training, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

95. Parittā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

95. Limited states are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

96. Appamāṇā [M 0.0017] dhammā… paṇītā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0020] asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

96. Immeasurable states and sublime states, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

97. Parittārammaṇā parittāramaṇā (?) dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

97. States with limited objects are included in four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

98. Mahaggatārammaṇā dhammā… appamāṇārammaṇā dhammā… hīnā dhammā… micchattaniyatā dhammā… sammattaniyatā dhammā… maggārammaṇā dhammā… maggahetukā dhammā… maggādhipatino dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

98. States with exalted objects, states with immeasurable objects, inferior states, states fixed in wrongness, states fixed in rightness, states with the path as object, states with the path as their cause, and states with the path as predominance are included in four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

99. Majjhimā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi [T 0.0020] dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

99. Intermediate states are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

100. Aniyatā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

100. Indeterminate states, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

101. Uppannā [V 0.0019] dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

101. Arisen states are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

102. Anuppannā [P 0.0021] dhammā pañcahi khandhehi sattahāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi pañcahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

102. Unarisen states are included in five aggregates, seven bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates; they are not included by five bases and ten elements.

103. Uppādino [M 0.0018] dhammā pañcahi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

103. States that are bound to arise are included in five aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates; they are not included by one base and one element.

104. Atītā dhammā… anāgatā dhammā… paccuppannā dhammā… ajjhattā dhammā… ajjhattabahiddhā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

104. Past states, future states, present states, internal states, and internal and external states are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

105. Bahiddhā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi [T 0.0021] dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

105. External states, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

106. Atītārammaṇā dhammā… anāgatārammaṇā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

106. States with past objects and states with future objects are included in four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

107. Paccuppannārammaṇā dhammā… ajjhattārammaṇā dhammā… bahiddhārammaṇā dhammā… ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena [P 0.0022] khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

107. States with present objects... states with internal objects... states with external objects... states with internal and external objects are included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements.

108. Sanidassanasappaṭighā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā[V 0.0020]. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

108. Visible and resistant states are included by one aggregate, one sense base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

109. Anidassanasappaṭighā dhammā ekena khandhena navahāyatanehi navahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi tīhāyatanehi navahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

109. Invisible and resistant states are included by one aggregate, nine sense bases, and nine elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, three sense bases, and nine elements.

110. Anidassanaappaṭighā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi, dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

110. Invisible and non-resistant states, excluding the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by any aggregates; they are not included by ten sense bases and ten elements.

8. Dukaṃ

8. The Dyads

111. Hetū [T 0.0022][M 0.0019] dhammā… hetū ceva sahetukā ca dhammā… hetū ceva hetusampayuttā ca dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

111. Phenomena that are roots… phenomena that are both roots and have roots… phenomena that are both roots and are associated with roots are included by one aggregate, one sense base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

112. Na hetū dhammā… ahetukā dhammā… hetuvippayuttā dhammā… na hetū ahetukā na hetū ahetukā (syā. ka.) vibha. dukapañhāpucchakepi dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

112. Phenomena that are not roots… phenomena that are rootless… phenomena that are dissociated from roots… phenomena that are not roots and are rootless, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by any aggregates, by any sense bases, or by any elements.

113. Sahetukā dhammā… hetusampayuttā dhammā… sahetukā ceva na ca hetū dhammā… hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū dhammā… na hetū sahetukā dhammā catūhi [P 0.0023] khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

113. Phenomena that have roots… phenomena that are associated with roots… phenomena that have roots but are not roots… phenomena that are associated with roots but are not roots… phenomena that are not roots but have roots are included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

114. Sappaccayā dhammā… saṅkhatā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi [V 0.0021] saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

114. Phenomena that have conditions… conditioned phenomena are included by five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by any aggregates, by any sense bases, or by any elements.

115. Appaccayā dhammā… asaṅkhatā dhammā na kehici khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Pañcahi [T 0.0023] khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

115. Phenomena without conditions… unconditioned phenomena are not included by any aggregates, but are included by one sense base and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by five aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

116. Sanidassanā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

116. Visible phenomena are included by one aggregate, one sense base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

117. Anidassanā [M 0.0020] dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi, ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

117. Invisible phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by any aggregates; they are not included by one sense base and one element.

118. Sappaṭighā dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

118. Phenomena with resistance are included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements.

119. Appaṭighā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ [P 0.0024] khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi, dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

119. Phenomena without resistance, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by any aggregates; they are not included by ten sense bases and ten elements.

120. Rūpino dhammā ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

120. Material phenomena are included by one aggregate, eleven sense bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, one sense base, and seven elements.

121. Arūpino dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

121. Immaterial phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements.

122. Lokiyā [T 0.0024][V 0.0022] dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

122. Mundane phenomena are included by five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by any aggregates, by any sense bases, or by any elements.

123. Lokuttarā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

123. Supramundane phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

124. Kenaci viññeyyā dhammā… kenaci na viññeyyā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

124. Phenomena cognizable by some consciousness… phenomena not cognizable by some consciousness, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by any aggregates, by any sense bases, or by any elements.

125. Āsavā [M 0.0021] dhammā… āsavā ceva sāsavā ca dhammā… āsavā ceva āsavasampayuttā ca dhammā ekena [P 0.0025] khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

125. Phenomena that are taints… phenomena that are both taints and subject to taints… phenomena that are both taints and associated with taints are included by one aggregate, one sense base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

126. No āsavā dhammā… āsavavippayuttā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

126. Phenomena that are not taints… phenomena dissociated from taints, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by any aggregates, by any sense bases, or by any elements.

127. Sāsavā dhammā… sāsavā ceva no ca āsavā dhammā… āsavavippayuttā sāsavā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi [T 0.0025] aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

127. Phenomena that are subject to taints… phenomena that are subject to taints but are not taints… phenomena dissociated from taints but subject to taints are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they un-included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, not un-included by any sense bases, and not un-included by any elements.

128. Anāsavā dhammā… āsavavippayuttā anāsavā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

128. Phenomena not subject to taints… phenomena dissociated from taints and not subject to taints, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

129. Āsavasampayuttā [V 0.0023] dhammā… āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

129. Phenomena associated with taints… phenomena that are associated with taints but are not taints are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

130. Saṃyojanā dhammā… ganthā dhammā… oghā dhammā… yogā dhammā… nīvaraṇā dhammā… parāmāsā dhammā… parāmāsā ceva parāmaṭṭhā ca dhammā ekena [P 0.0026] khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

130. Phenomena that are fetters... phenomena that are bonds... phenomena that are floods... phenomena that are yokes... phenomena that are hindrances... phenomena that are grasping... phenomena that are both grasping and objects of grasping are included in one aggregate, one sense base, and one element. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

131. No parāmāsā dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā[M 0.0022]. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

131. Phenomena that are not grasping... phenomena dissociated from grasping, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they un-included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, not un-included by any sense bases, and not un-included by any elements.

132. Parāmaṭṭhā dhammā… parāmaṭṭhā ceva no ca parāmāsā dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttā parāmaṭṭhā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

132. Phenomena that are objects of grasping... phenomena that are objects of grasping but are not grasping... phenomena dissociated from grasping that are objects of grasping are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they un-included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, not un-included by any sense bases, and not un-included by any elements.

133. Aparāmaṭṭhā dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttā aparāmaṭṭhā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

133. Phenomena that are not objects of grasping... phenomena dissociated from grasping that are not objects of grasping, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

134. Parāmāsasampayuttā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

134. Phenomena associated with grasping are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

135. Sārammaṇā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

135. Phenomena that have an object are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements.

136. Anārammaṇā [P 0.0027] dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi [V 0.0024] dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

136. Phenomena that are without an object, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in one aggregate, eleven sense bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by four aggregates, one sense base, and seven elements.

137. Cittā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

137. Phenomena that are consciousness are included in one aggregate, one sense base, and seven elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and eleven elements.

138. No cittā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi [T 0.0027] ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

138. Phenomena that are not consciousness, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by one aggregate, one sense base, and seven elements.

139. Cetasikā [M 0.0023] dhammā… cittasampayuttā dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhā dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

139. Phenomena that are mental factors... phenomena associated with consciousness... phenomena conjoined with consciousness are included in three aggregates, one sense base, and one element. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by two aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

140. Acetasikā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā dvīhi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

140. Phenomena that are not mental factors, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in two aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by three aggregates; they are not un-included by any sense bases and not un-included by any elements.

141. Cittavippayuttā dhammā… cittavisaṃsaṭṭhā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

141. Phenomena dissociated from consciousness... phenomena unconjoined with consciousness, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in one aggregate, eleven sense bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by four aggregates, one sense base, and seven elements.

142. Cittasamuṭṭhānā [P 0.0028] dhammā catūhi khandhehi chahāyatanehi chahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena chahāyatanehi dvādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

142. Phenomena originated from consciousness are included in four aggregates, six sense bases, and six elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by one aggregate, six sense bases, and twelve elements.

143. No cittasamuṭṭhānā dhammā… no cittasahabhuno dhammā… no cittānuparivattino dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā dvīhi khandhehi [T 0.0028] dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

143. Phenomena not originated from consciousness... phenomena not co-arisen with consciousness... phenomena not arising in conformity with consciousness, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in two aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by three aggregates; they are not un-included by any sense bases and not un-included by any elements.

144. Cittasahabhuno [V 0.0025] dhammā… cittānuparivattino dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

144. Phenomena co-arisen with consciousness... phenomena arising in conformity with consciousness are included in four aggregates, one sense base, and one element. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by one aggregate, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

145. Cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

145. Phenomena conjoined with and originated from consciousness... phenomena conjoined with, originated from, and co-arisen with consciousness... phenomena conjoined with, originated from, and arising in conformity with consciousness are included in three aggregates, one sense base, and one element. By how many are they un-included? They are un-included by two aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

146. No [M 0.0024] cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā dhammā… no cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno dhammā… no cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā dvīhi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

146. Phenomena neither conjoined with nor originated from consciousness... phenomena neither conjoined with, originated from, nor co-arisen with consciousness... phenomena neither conjoined with, originated from, nor arising in conformity with consciousness, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by two aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates; they are not un-included by any bases, nor by any elements.

147. Ajjhattikā dhammā dvīhi [P 0.0029] khandhehi chahāyatanehi dvādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Tīhi khandhehi chahāyatanehi chahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

147. Internal phenomena are included by two aggregates, six bases, and twelve elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by three aggregates, six bases, and six elements.

148. Bāhirā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi chahāyatanehi chahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena [T 0.0029] chahāyatanehi dvādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

148. External phenomena, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by four aggregates, six bases, and six elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, six bases, and twelve elements.

149. Upādā dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

149. Dependently arisen phenomena are included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements.

150. No upādā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi tīhāyatanehi navahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi navahāyatanehi navahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.
