150. Phenomena that are not dependently arisen, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by five aggregates, three bases, and nine elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, but are not included by nine bases and nine elements.

151. Upādinnā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā[V 0.0026]. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

151. Appropriated phenomena are included by five aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, but are not included by one base and one element.

152. Anupādinnā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi sattahāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi pañcahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

152. Unappropriated phenomena, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by five aggregates, seven bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, but are not included by five bases and ten elements.

153. Upādānā dhammā… kilesā dhammā… kilesā ceva saṃkilesikā ca dhammā… kilesā ceva saṃkiliṭṭhā ca dhammā… kilesā ceva kilesasampayuttā ca dhammā ekena [P 0.0030] khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā [M 0.0025] saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

153. Phenomena of clinging… defilements… phenomena that are both defilements and conducive to defilement… phenomena that are both defilements and defiled… phenomena that are both defilements and associated with defilements are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

154. No kilesā dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhā dhammā… kilesavippayuttā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi [T 0.0030] dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

154. Phenomena that are not defilements… undefiled phenomena… phenomena dissociated from defilements, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

155. Saṃkilesikā dhammā… saṃkilesikā ceva no ca kilesā dhammā… kilesavippayuttā saṃkilesikā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

155. Phenomena conducive to defilement… phenomena that are conducive to defilement but are not defilements… phenomena dissociated from defilements that are conducive to defilement are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

156. Asaṃkilesikā dhammā… kilesavippayuttā asaṃkilesikā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

156. Phenomena not conducive to defilement… phenomena dissociated from defilements and not conducive to defilement, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

157. Saṃkiliṭṭhā dhammā… kilesasampayuttā dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhā ceva no ca kilesā dhammā… kilesasampayuttā ceva no ca kilesā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi [V 0.0027] saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

157. Defiled phenomena… phenomena associated with defilements… phenomena that are defiled but are not defilements… phenomena that are associated with defilements but are not defilements are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

158. Dassanena pahātabbā dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā catūhi [P 0.0031] khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

158. Phenomena to be abandoned by vision… phenomena to be abandoned by development… phenomena with roots to be abandoned by vision… phenomena with roots to be abandoned by development are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

159. Na dassanena pahātabbā dhammā… na bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā [T 0.0031]… na dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā… na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi [M 0.0026] dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

159. Phenomena not to be abandoned by vision… phenomena not to be abandoned by development… phenomena whose roots are not to be abandoned by vision… phenomena whose roots are not to be abandoned by development, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

160. Savitakkā dhammā… savicārā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

160. Phenomena with initial application… phenomena with sustained application are included by four aggregates, two bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements.

161. Avitakkā dhammā… avicārā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

161. Phenomena without initial application… phenomena without sustained application, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, but are not included by one element.

162. Sappītikā dhammā… pītisahagatā dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

162. Phenomena with rapture… phenomena accompanied by rapture are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

163. Appītikā dhammā… na pītisahagatā dhammā… na sukhasahagatā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0032] asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

163. Phenomena without rapture… phenomena not accompanied by rapture… phenomena not accompanied by pleasure, excluding the unconditioned from the aggregate-count, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

164. Sukhasahagatā [V 0.0028] dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi [T 0.0032] dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

164. Phenomena accompanied by pleasure are included by three aggregates, two sense bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten sense bases, and fifteen elements.

165. Upekkhāsahagatā dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

165. Phenomena accompanied by equanimity are included by three aggregates, two sense bases, and seven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten sense bases, and eleven elements.

166. Na upekkhāsahagatā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi terasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

166. Phenomena not accompanied by equanimity, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not not-included by any aggregates, nor by any sense bases; they are not-included by five elements.

167. Kāmāvacarā [M 0.0027] dhammā… pariyāpannā dhammā… sauttarā dhammā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

167. Dhammas of the sense-sphere... included dhammas... dhammas with a superior are included by five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not not-included by any aggregates, nor by any sense bases, nor by any elements.

168. Na kāmāvacarā dhammā… apariyāpannā dhammā… anuttarā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

168. Dhammas not of the sense-sphere... un-included dhammas... unsurpassed dhammas—setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates—are included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

169. Rūpāvacarā dhammā… arūpāvacarā dhammā… niyyānikā dhammā… niyatā dhammā… saraṇā dhammā catūhi [P 0.0033] khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

169. Dhammas of the form-sphere... dhammas of the formless-sphere... dhammas that lead out... fixed dhammas... dhammas with defilements are included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

170. Na [T 0.0033] rūpāvacarā dhammā… na arūpāvacarā dhammā… aniyyānikā dhammā… aniyatā dhammā… araṇā dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Araṇā dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

170. Dhammas not of the form-sphere... dhammas not of the formless-sphere... dhammas that do not lead out... unfixed dhammas... dhammas without defilements—by how many aggregates, sense bases, and elements are they included? Dhammas without defilements, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not not-included by any aggregates, nor by any sense bases, nor by any elements.

Saṅgahāsaṅgahapadaniddeso paṭhamo.

The First Exposition of Terms on Inclusion and Non-inclusion.

2. Dutiyanayo

2. The Second Method

2. Saṅgahitenaasaṅgahitapadaniddeso

2. Exposition of Terms on the Un-included by the Included

171. Cakkhāyatanena [P 0.0034][M 0.0028][V 0.0029] ye dhammā… phoṭṭhabbāyatanena ye dhammā… cakkhudhātuyā ye dhammā… phoṭṭhabbadhātuyā ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā? Te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

171. [Regarding] the eye sense base, which dhammas... [regarding] the tangible sense base, which dhammas... [regarding] the eye element, which dhammas... [regarding] the tangible element, which dhammas are included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the sense-base-classification, and not included by the element-classification? By how many aggregates, sense bases, and elements are those dhammas not included? Those dhammas are not included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements.

172. Cakkhuviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… sotaviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… ghānaviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… jivhāviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… kāyaviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… manodhātuyā ye dhammā… manoviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena [T 0.0034] asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi dvādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

172. [Regarding] the eye-consciousness-element, which dhammas... [regarding] the ear-consciousness-element, which dhammas... [regarding] the nose-consciousness-element, which dhammas... [regarding] the tongue-consciousness-element, which dhammas... [regarding] the body-consciousness-element, which dhammas... [regarding] the mind-element, which dhammas... [regarding] the mind-consciousness-element, which dhammas are included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the sense-base-classification, and not included by the element-classification... and so on... those dhammas are not included by four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and twelve elements.

173. Cakkhundriyena ye dhammā… sotindriyena ye dhammā… ghānindriyena ye dhammā… jivhindriyena ye dhammā… kāyindriyena ye dhammā… itthindriyena ye dhammā… purisindriyena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

173. [Regarding] the eye faculty, which dhammas... [regarding] the ear faculty, which dhammas... [regarding] the nose faculty, which dhammas... [regarding] the tongue faculty, which dhammas... [regarding] the body faculty, which dhammas... [regarding] the female faculty, which dhammas are included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the sense-base-classification, and not included by the element-classification... and so on... those dhammas are not included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements.

174. Asaññābhavena ye dhammā… ekavokārabhavena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā catūhi [P 0.0035] khandhehi tīhāyatanehi navahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

174. [Regarding] the non-percipient existence, which dhammas... [regarding] the one-aggregate existence, which dhammas are included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the sense-base-classification, and not included by the element-classification... and so on... those dhammas are not included by four aggregates, three sense bases, and nine elements.

175. Paridevena ye dhammā… sanidassanasappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

175. [Regarding] lamentation, which dhammas... [regarding] dhammas that are visible and resistant, which dhammas are included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the sense-base-classification, and not included by the element-classification... and so on... those dhammas are not included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements.

176. Anidassanasappaṭighehi [M 0.0029] dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

176. [Regarding] dhammas that are invisible and resistant, which dhammas are included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the sense-base-classification, and not included by the element-classification... and so on... those dhammas are not included by four aggregates, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

177. Sanidassanehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena [T 0.0035] asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

177. [Regarding] visible dhammas, which dhammas are included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the sense-base-classification, and not included by the element-classification... and so on... those dhammas are not included by four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements.

178. Sappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādādhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā? Te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

178. [Regarding] resistant dhammas, which dhammas... [regarding] dependent dhammas, which dhammas are included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the sense-base-classification, and not included by the element-classification? By how many aggregates, sense bases, and elements are those dhammas not included? Those dhammas are not included by four aggregates, eleven sense bases, and seventeen elements.

Dasāyatanā sattarasa dhātuyo,

Sattindriyā asaññābhavo ekavokārabhavo;

Paridevo sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ,

Anidassanaṃ punadeva punareva (pī.) sappaṭighaṃ upādāti.

Ten sense bases, seventeen elements; seven faculties, non-percipient existence, one-aggregate existence; lamentation, the visible-and-resistant, the invisible, and again the resistant, and the dependent.

Saṅgahitenaasaṅgahitapadaniddeso dutiyo.

The Second Exposition of Terms on the Un-included by the Included.

3. Tatiyanayo

3. The Third Method

3. Asaṅgahitenasaṅgahitapadaniddeso

3. Exposition of Terms on the Included by the Un-included

179. Vedanākkhandhena [P 0.0036][M 0.0030][V 0.0031] ye dhammā… saññākkhandhena ye dhammā… saṅkhārakkhandhena ye dhammā… samudayasaccena ye dhammā… maggasaccena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

179. With respect to the feeling aggregate, which phenomena… with respect to the perception aggregate, which phenomena… with respect to the formations aggregate, which phenomena… with respect to the truth of origination, which phenomena… with respect to the truth of the path, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification? By how many aggregates, by how many bases, and by how many elements are those phenomena included? Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-counting, are included by three aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

180. Nirodhasaccena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena [T 0.0036] saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

180. With respect to the truth of cessation, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena are included by four aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

181. Jīvitindriyena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

181. With respect to the life faculty, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-counting, are included by two aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

182. Itthindriyena ye dhammā… purisindriyena ye dhammā… sukhindriyena ye dhammā… dukkhindriyena ye dhammā… somanassindriyena ye dhammā… domanassindriyena ye dhammā… upekkhindriyena ye dhammā… saddhindriyena ye dhammā… vīriyindriyena ye dhammā… satindriyena ye dhammā… samādhindriyena ye dhammā… paññindriyena ye dhammā… anaññātaññassāmītindriyena ye dhammā… aññindriyena ye dhammā… aññātāvindriyena ye dhammā… avijjāya ye dhammā… avijjāpaccayā [V 0.0032] saṅkhārena ye dhammā… saḷāyatanapaccayā phassena ye dhammā… phassapaccayā vedanāya ye dhammā… vedanāpaccayā [P 0.0037] taṇhāya ye dhammā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānena ye dhammā… kammabhavena upādānapaccayā kammabhavena (syā.) ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

182. With respect to the female faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the male faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the pleasure faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the pain faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the joy faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the displeasure faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the equanimity faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the faith faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the energy faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the mindfulness faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the concentration faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the wisdom faculty, which phenomena… with respect to the faculty of ‘I shall know the unknown,’ which phenomena… with respect to the faculty of knowledge, which phenomena… with respect to the faculty of one who has final knowledge, which phenomena… with respect to ignorance, which phenomena… with respect to formations conditioned by ignorance, which phenomena… with respect to contact conditioned by the six sense bases, which phenomena… with respect to feeling conditioned by contact, which phenomena… with respect to craving conditioned by feeling, which phenomena… with respect to clinging conditioned by craving, which phenomena… with respect to kamma-becoming, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-counting, are included by three aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

183. Jātiyā [T 0.0037][M 0.0031] ye dhammā… jarāya ye dhammā… maraṇena ye dhammā… jhānena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

183. With respect to birth, which phenomena… with respect to aging, which phenomena… with respect to death, which phenomena… with respect to jhāna, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-counting, are included by two aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

184. Sokena ye dhammā… dukkhena ye dhammā… domanassena ye dhammā… upāyāsena ye dhammā… satipaṭṭhānena ye dhammā… sammappadhānena ye dhammā… appamaññāya ye dhammā… pañcahi indriyehi ye dhammā… pañcahi balehi ye dhammā… sattahi bojjhaṅgehi ye dhammā… ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena ye dhammā… phassena ye dhammā… vedanāya ye dhammā… saññāya ye dhammā… cetanāya ye dhammā… adhimokkhena ye dhammā… manasikārena ye dhammā… hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva sahetukehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva hetusampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

184. With respect to sorrow, which phenomena… with respect to suffering… with respect to grief… with respect to despair… with respect to the foundations of mindfulness… with respect to the right efforts… with respect to the immeasurables… with respect to the five faculties… with respect to the five powers… with respect to the seven factors of awakening… with respect to the Noble Eightfold Path… with respect to contact… with respect to feeling… with respect to perception… with respect to volition… with respect to resolution… with respect to attention… with respect to phenomena that are roots… with respect to phenomena that are both roots and have roots… with respect to phenomena that are both roots and associated with roots, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-counting, are included by three aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

185. Appaccayehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṅkhatehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā [T 0.0038] saṅgahitā.

185. With respect to phenomena without conditions, which phenomena… with respect to unconditioned phenomena, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena are included by four aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

186. Āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva sāsavehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva āsavasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ [P 0.0038] khandhato ṭhapetvā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

186. With respect to phenomena that are effluents, which phenomena… with respect to phenomena that are both effluents and subject to effluents… with respect to phenomena that are both effluents and associated with effluents, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-counting, are included by three aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

187. Saṃyojanehi [V 0.0033] … ganthehi… oghehi… yogehi… nīvaraṇehi… parāmāsehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi ceva parāmaṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

187. With respect to fetters… knots… floods… yokes… hindrances… attachments, which phenomena… with respect to phenomena that are both attachments and subject to attachment, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-counting, are included by three aggregates, by one base, and by one element.

188. Cetasikehi [M 0.0032] dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

188. With respect to phenomena that are mental concomitants, which phenomena… with respect to phenomena associated with consciousness… with respect to phenomena intermingled with consciousness… with respect to phenomena arising from intermingling with consciousness… with respect to phenomena co-arising with what arises from intermingling with consciousness… with respect to phenomena occurring in conformity with what arises from intermingling with consciousness, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and by the element classification… Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-counting, are included by one aggregate, by one base, and by one element.

189. Cittasahabhūmi [T 0.0039] dhammehi ye dhammā… cittānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

189. By those phenomena that are co-nascent with consciousness, which phenomena... by those phenomena that arise in sequence to consciousness, which phenomena are not included by way of the aggregate classification, but are included by way of the base classification and included by way of the element classification... Those phenomena are not included by any aggregates, but are included by one base and one element.

190. Upādānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkilesikehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkiliṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva kilesasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

190. By those phenomena that are clingings, which phenomena... by those phenomena that are defilements... by those phenomena that are both defilements and defiling... by those phenomena that are both defilements and defiled... by those phenomena that are both defilements and associated with defilements—which are not included by way of the aggregate classification, but are included by way of the base classification and the element classification—by how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena included? Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the aggregate classification, are included by three aggregates, one base, and one element.

Tayo khandhā tathā saccā, indriyāni ca soḷasa;

Padāni paccayākāre, cuddasūpari cuddasa.

Three aggregates, and likewise the truths, and sixteen faculties; terms in the mode of conditions, fourteen and fourteen.

Samatiṃsa padā honti, gocchakesu dasasvatha;

Duve cūḷantaradukā cullantaradukā (sī.), aṭṭha honti mahantarāti.

There are thirty terms, and ten in the clusters; two are minor intermediate dyads, and eight are major intermediate dyads.

Asaṅgahitenasaṅgahitapadaniddeso tatiyo.

The third is the Exposition of Terms Included by the Not-Included.

4. Catutthanayo

4. The Fourth Method

4. Saṅgahitenasaṅgahitapadaniddeso

4. Exposition of Terms Included by the Included

191. Samudayasaccena [P 0.0039][M 0.0033][V 0.0034] ye dhammā… maggasaccena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi [T 0.0040] katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

191. By the truth of origin, which phenomena... by the truth of the path, which phenomena are included by way of the aggregate classification, the base classification, and the element classification; and by those phenomena, which phenomena are included by way of the aggregate classification, the base classification, and the element classification—by how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena included? Those phenomena are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element.

192. Itthindriyena ye dhammā… purisindriyena ye dhammā… sukhindriyena ye dhammā… dukkhindriyena ye dhammā… somanassindriyena ye dhammā… domanassindriyena ye dhammā… upekkhindriyena ye dhammā… saddhindriyena ye dhammā… vīriyindriyena ye dhammā… satindriyena ye dhammā… samādhindriyena ye dhammā… paññindriyena ye dhammā… anaññātaññassāmītindriyena ye dhammā… aññindriyena ye dhammā… aññātāvindriyena ye dhammā….

192. By the female faculty, which phenomena... by the male faculty, which phenomena... by the faculty of pleasure, which phenomena... by the faculty of pain, which phenomena... by the faculty of joy, which phenomena... by the faculty of displeasure, which phenomena... by the faculty of equanimity, which phenomena... by the faculty of faith, which phenomena... by the faculty of energy, which phenomena... by the faculty of mindfulness, which phenomena... by the faculty of concentration, which phenomena... by the faculty of wisdom, which phenomena... by the faculty of 'I shall know the unknown', which phenomena... by the faculty of final knowledge, which phenomena... by the faculty of one who has final knowledge, which phenomena...

Avijjāya ye dhammā… avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārena ye dhammā… saḷāyatanapaccayā phassena ye dhammā… vedanāpaccayā taṇhāya ye dhammā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānena ye dhammā… kammabhavena ye dhammā… sokena ye dhammā… paridevena ye dhammā… dukkhena ye dhammā… domanassena ye dhammā… upāyāsena ye dhammā….

By ignorance, which phenomena... by formations conditioned by ignorance, which phenomena... by contact conditioned by the six sense bases, which phenomena... by craving conditioned by feeling, which phenomena... by clinging conditioned by craving, which phenomena... by kamma-becoming, which phenomena... by sorrow, which phenomena... by lamentation, which phenomena... by pain, which phenomena... by displeasure, which phenomena... by despair, which phenomena...

Satipaṭṭhānena ye dhammā… sammappadhānena ye dhammā… appamaññāya ye dhammā… pañcahi indriyehi ye dhammā… pañcahi balehi ye dhammā… sattahi bojjhaṅgehi ye dhammā… ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena [V 0.0035] maggena ye dhammā… phassena ye dhammā… cetanāya ye dhammā… adhimokkhena ye dhammā… manasikārena ye dhammā [P 0.0040]….

By the foundations of mindfulness, which phenomena... by the right strivings, which phenomena... by the immeasurables, which phenomena... by the five faculties, which phenomena... by the five powers, which phenomena... by the seven factors of enlightenment, which phenomena... by the Noble Eightfold Path, which phenomena... by contact, which phenomena... by volition, which phenomena... by resolve, which phenomena... by attention, which phenomena...

Hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva sahetukehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva hetusampayuttehi ca dhammehi [T 0.0041] ye dhammā… āsavehi… saṃyojanehi… ganthehi… oghehi… yogehi… nīvaraṇehi… parāmāsehi… upādānehi… kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva [M 0.0034] saṃkilesikehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkiliṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva kilesasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā.

By phenomena that are roots... by phenomena that are both roots and have roots... by phenomena that are both roots and associated with roots... by taints... fetters... bonds... floods... yokes... hindrances... misapprehensions... clingings... by phenomena that are defilements... by phenomena that are both defilements and defiling... by phenomena that are both defilements and defiled... by phenomena that are both defilements and associated with defilements—which are included by way of the aggregate classification, the base classification, and the element classification; and by those phenomena, which phenomena are included by way of the aggregate classification, the base classification, and the element classification—by how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena included? Those phenomena are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element.

Dve saccā pannarasindriyā, ekādasa paṭiccapadā;

Uddhaṃ puna ekādasa, gocchakapadamettha tiṃsavidhāti tiṃsavidhanti (pī.).

Two truths, fifteen faculties, eleven terms of dependent origination; and another eleven above. Here the cluster-terms are thirtyfold.

Saṅgahitenasaṅgahitapadaniddeso catuttho.

The fourth is the Exposition of Terms Included by the Included.

5. Pañcamanayo

5. The Fifth Method

193. Rūpakkhandhena [P 0.0041][M 0.0035][V 0.0036] ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi [T 0.0042] dhātūhi asaṅgahitā? Te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

193. By the form aggregate, which phenomena are not included by way of the aggregate classification, not included by way of the base classification, and not included by way of the element classification; and by those phenomena, which phenomena are not included by way of the aggregate classification, not included by way of the base classification, and not included by way of the element classification—by how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena not included? Those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements.

194. Vedanākkhandhena ye dhammā… saññākkhandhena ye dhammā… saṅkhārakkhandhena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

194. Those phenomena are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

195. 195. By those phenomena, which phenomena… and so on… those phenomena are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements.

196. 197. 199. Cakkhundriyena ye dhammā…pe… kāyindriyena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

200. 204. 205. By those phenomena, which phenomena… and so on… those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

206. By those phenomena, which phenomena… and so on… those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements.

207. Paridevena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

209. Cittena [M 0.0038] ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

1. Tikaṃ

1. The Triad.

210. Kusalehi dhammehi ye dhammā… akusalehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… vipākehi dhammehi ye dhammā… vipākadhammadhammehi ye dhammā… anupādinnaanupādāniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikehi [P 0.0045] dhammehi ye dhammā… savitakkasavicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā… avitakkavicāramattehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye [T 0.0047] dhammā… ācayagāmīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… apacayagāmīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… sekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… mahaggatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… appamāṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parittārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… mahaggatārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… appamāṇārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hīnehi dhammehi ye dhammā… paṇītehi dhammehi ye dhammā… micchattaniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sammattaniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggādhipatīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… atītārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anāgatārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… paccupannārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bahiddhārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena [V 0.0040] asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

210. By phenomena that are wholesome; unwholesome; associated with pleasant feeling; associated with painful feeling; associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling; resultant; that yield resultants; unappropriated and not objects of clinging; defiled and defiling; undefiled and non-defiling; accompanied by applied and sustained thought; without applied thought but with only sustained thought; accompanied by joy; accompanied by happiness; accompanied by equanimity; to be abandoned by seeing; to be abandoned by development; with roots to be abandoned by seeing; with roots to be abandoned by development; leading to accumulation; leading to decline; of a trainee; of one beyond training; exalted; immeasurable; with limited objects; with exalted objects; with immeasurable objects; inferior; sublime; fixed on wrongness; fixed on rightness; with the path as object; with the path as root; with the path as dominant factor; with past objects; with future objects; with present objects; with internal objects; with external objects; or with both internal and external objects—which phenomena are not included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the base-classification, and not included by the element-classification; by those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

211. Sanidassanasappaṭighehi [M 0.0039] dhammehi ye dhammā… anidassanasappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā[T 0.0048].

211. By phenomena that are visible and resistant... by phenomena that are invisible and resistant, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the base-classification, and not included by the element-classification; by those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements.

2. Dukaṃ

2. The Dyad.

212. Hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva sahetukehi ca dhammehi [P 0.0046] ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva hetusampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

212. By phenomena that are roots... by phenomena that are both roots and have roots... by phenomena that are both roots and are associated with roots: which phenomena are not included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the base-classification, not included by the element-classification; by those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

213. Sahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sahetukehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… na hetusahetukehi na hetū sahetukehi (sī.), na hetūhi sahetukehi (syā. ka.) dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

213. By phenomena that have roots... by phenomena associated with roots... by phenomena that have roots but are not roots... by phenomena associated with roots but are not roots... by phenomena that are not roots but have roots: which phenomena are not included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the base-classification, not included by the element-classification; by those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

214. Appaccayehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṅkhatehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

214. By phenomena that are without condition... by phenomena that are uncompounded, which phenomena are not included by the aggregate-classification, not included by the base-classification, and not included by the element-classification; by those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

215. Sanidassanehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā catūhi [T 0.0049] khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

215. By phenomena with manifestation, and by phenomena with resistance: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements.

216. Rūpīhi [V 0.0041] dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye [M 0.0040] dhammā…pe… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

216. By material phenomena: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements.

217. Arūpīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… lokuttarehi dhammehi [P 0.0047] ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

217. By immaterial phenomena, and by supramundane phenomena: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

218. Āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva sāsavehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva āsavasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

218. By phenomena that are taints... by phenomena that are both taints and subject to taints... by phenomena that are both taints and associated with taints: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

219. Anāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavasampayuttehi ceva no ca āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavavippayuttehi anāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

219. By phenomena that are taintless... by phenomena associated with taints... by phenomena associated with taints but are not taints... by phenomena dissociated from taints and taintless: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

220. Saṃyojanehi [T 0.0050] dhammehi ye dhammā… ganthehi dhammehi ye dhammā… oghehi dhammehi ye dhammā… yogehi dhammehi ye dhammā… nīvaraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi ceva parāmaṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

220. By phenomena that are fetters... by phenomena that are bonds... by phenomena that are floods... by phenomena that are yokes... by phenomena that are hindrances... by phenomena that are misapprehensions... by phenomena that are both misapprehensions and misapprehended: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

221. Aparāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttehi aparāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā [V 0.0042] āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā[P 0.0048], tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

221. By phenomena that are not misapprehended... by phenomena associated with misapprehension... by phenomena dissociated from misapprehension and not misapprehended... by phenomena that have an object: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

222. Anārammaṇehi [M 0.0041] dhammehi ye dhammā… no cittehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittavippayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittavisaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasahabhūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… bāhirehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādādhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi [T 0.0051] dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

222. By phenomena without an object... by phenomena that are not mind... by phenomena dissociated from mind... by phenomena unconjoined with mind... by phenomena originated by mind... by phenomena co-existent with mind... by phenomena consequent upon mind... by external phenomena... by phenomena that are clingings: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements.

223. Cittehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

223. By phenomena that are mind: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements.

224. Cetasikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

224. By phenomena that are mental factors... by phenomena associated with mind... by phenomena conjoined with mind... by phenomena conjoined with and originated from mind... by phenomena conjoined with, originated from, and co-existent with mind... by phenomena conjoined with, originated from, and consequent upon mind: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

225. Ajjhattikehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

225. By internal phenomena: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, and not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by three aggregates, one base, and one element.

226. Upādānehi [P 0.0049] dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkilesikehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkiliṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva kilesasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena [T 0.0052][V 0.0043] asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā…pe… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

226. By phenomena that are clingings... by phenomena that are defilements... by phenomena that are both defilements and liable to defilement... by phenomena that are both defilements and defiled... by phenomena that are both defilements and associated with defilements: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, not included by the reckoning of bases, not included by the reckoning of elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena... (etc.)... those phenomena are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

227. Asaṃkilesikehi [M 0.0042] dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesavippayuttehi asaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savitakkehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sappītikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… na kāmāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… rūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… arūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… apariyāpannehi dhammehi ye dhammā… niyyānikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… niyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anuttarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena [P 0.0050] asaṅgahitā, te dhammā [T 0.0053] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā? Te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

227. Regarding phenomena that are not liable to defilement... defiled phenomena... phenomena associated with defilements... phenomena that are defiled but are not defilements... phenomena associated with defilements but not defilements... phenomena dissociated from defilements and not liable to defilement... phenomena to be abandoned by vision... phenomena to be abandoned by development... phenomena with causes to be abandoned by vision... phenomena with causes to be abandoned by development... phenomena accompanied by initial application... phenomena accompanied by sustained application... phenomena accompanied by rapture... phenomena accompanied by rapture... phenomena accompanied by happiness... phenomena accompanied by equanimity... phenomena not of the sense-sphere... phenomena of the form-sphere... phenomena of the formless-sphere... phenomena not comprised in the round of existence... phenomena that lead out... phenomena that are fixed... phenomena that are unsurpassed... phenomena with defilements: which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, bases, and elements? By those phenomena, which phenomena are not included by the reckoning of aggregates, bases, and elements? By how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena not included? Those phenomena are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

Rūpañca dhammāyatanaṃ dhammadhātu, itthipumaṃ jīvitaṃ nāmarūpaṃ;

Dve bhavā jāti jarā maccurūpaṃ, anārammaṇaṃ no cittaṃ cittena vippayuttaṃ.

The form aggregate, the dhamma-base, and the dhamma-element; the femininity faculty and the masculinity faculty, the life faculty, name-and-form; the two kinds of existence, birth, aging, death, the term 'material'; the term 'without an object', the term 'not consciousness', the term 'dissociated from consciousness'.

Visaṃsaṭṭhaṃ samuṭṭhāna-sahabhu anuparivatti;

Bāhiraṃ upādā dve, visayo dvevīsati (syā.) esanayo subuddho.

The term 'unconjoined with consciousness', the term 'originated from consciousness', the term 'co-arisen with consciousness', the term 'consecutively occurring with consciousness'; the two terms 'external' and 'derived'; thus this method, the domain, is well-understood.

Asaṅgahitenaasaṅgahitapadaniddeso pañcamo.

The Fifth Exposition of the Term 'Not Included' by the 'Not Included'.

6. Chaṭṭhanayo

6. The Sixth Method

6. Sampayogavippayogapadaniddeso

6. Exposition of Terms on Association and Dissociation

1. Khandho

1. The Aggregates

228. Rūpakkhandho [P 0.0051][M 0.0043][V 0.0044] katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttoti? Natthi. Katihi vippayutto? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

228. The aggregate of form—with how many aggregates, bases, and elements is it associated? With none. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

229. Vedanākkhandho… saññākkhandho… saṅkhārakkhandho tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayutto. Katihi vippayutto? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

229. The aggregate of feeling… the aggregate of perception… the aggregate of formations are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and with some part of one base and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

230. Viññāṇakkhandho [T 0.0054] tīhi khandhehi sampayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayutto. Katihi vippayutto? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

230. The aggregate of consciousness is associated with three aggregates; and with some part of one base and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

2. Āyatanaṃ

2. The Bases

231. Cakkhāyatanaṃ…pe… phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ…pe… sampayuttanti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttaṃ[V 0.0045]? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

231. The eye-base… the tangible-base—are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

232. Manāyatanaṃ tīhi khandhehi sampayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena [P 0.0052] ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

232. The mind-base is associated with three aggregates; and with some part of one base and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

3. Dhātu

3. The Elements

233. Cakkhudhātu [M 0.0044] …pe… phoṭṭhabbadhātu…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

233. The eye-element… the tangible-element—are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

234. Cakkhuviññāṇadhātu…pe… manodhātu… manoviññāṇadhātu tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

234. The eye-consciousness-element… the mind-element… the mind-consciousness-element are associated with three aggregates; and with some part of one base and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

4. Saccādi

4. Truths, etc.

235. Samudayasaccaṃ… maggasaccaṃ tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya [T 0.0055] dhātuyā sampayuttaṃ; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

235. The truth of origin… the truth of the path are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and with some part of one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

236. Nirodhasaccaṃ… cakkhundriyaṃ…pe… kāyindriyaṃ… itthindriyaṃ… purisindriyaṃ…pe… sampayuttanti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

236. The truth of cessation… the eye faculty… the body faculty… the femininity faculty… the masculinity faculty… are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

237. Manindriyaṃ [V 0.0046] tīhi khandhehi sampayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

237. The mind faculty is associated with three aggregates; and with some part of one base and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and from some part of one base and one element.

238. Sukhindriyaṃ… dukkhindriyaṃ… somanassindriyaṃ… domanassindriyaṃ tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena [P 0.0053] khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

238. The pleasure faculty… the pain faculty… the joy faculty… the grief faculty are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and with some aspects of one base and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

239. Upekkhindriyaṃ [M 0.0045] tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena chahi dhātūhi sampayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ[T 0.0056]; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

239. The equanimity faculty is associated with three aggregates, one base, and six elements; and with some aspects of one base and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and eleven elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

240. Saddhindriyaṃ… vīriyindriyaṃ… satindriyaṃ… samādhindriyaṃ… paññindriyaṃ… anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṃ… aññindriyaṃ… aññātāvindriyaṃ… avijjā… avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

240. The faculty of faith… the faculty of energy… the faculty of mindfulness… the faculty of concentration… the faculty of wisdom… the faculty of I-shall-come-to-know-the-unknown… the faculty of final knowledge… the faculty of one who has final knowledge… ignorance… formations conditioned by ignorance are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and with some aspects of one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

241. Saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇaṃ tīhi khandhehi sampayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

241. Consciousness conditioned by formations is associated with three aggregates; and with some aspects of one base and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

242. Saḷāyatanapaccayā phasso tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayutto; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayutto. Katihi vippayutto? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

242. Contact conditioned by the sixfold base is associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and with some aspects of one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

243. Phassapaccayā [V 0.0047] vedanā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā[T 0.0057]; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

243. Feeling conditioned by contact is associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and with some aspects of one base and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

244. Vedanāpaccayā taṇhā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānaṃ… kammabhavo tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayutto; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayutto. Katihi vippayutto[M 0.0046]? Ekena [P 0.0054] khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

244. Craving conditioned by feeling… clinging conditioned by craving… kamma-becoming is associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and with some aspects of one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

245. Rūpabhavo…pe… sampayuttoti? Natthi. Katihi vippayutto? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi vippayutto.

245. Form-becoming… is it associated? There is no association. With how many is it dissociated? It is not dissociated from any aggregates nor from any bases; it is dissociated from three elements.

246. Arūpabhavo… nevasaññānāsaññābhavo… catuvokārabhavo…pe… sampayuttoti? Natthi. Katihi vippayutto? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

246. Formless becoming… the becoming of neither-perception-nor-non-perception… four-constituent becoming… are they associated? There is no association. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

247. Asaññābhavo… ekavokārabhavo… paridevo…pe… sampayuttoti? Natthi. Katihi vippayutto? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

247. Non-percipient becoming… one-constituent becoming… lamentation… are they associated? There is no association. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

248. Soko… dukkhaṃ… domanassaṃ tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi [T 0.0058] dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

248. Sorrow… pain… grief are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and with some aspects of one base and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

249. Upāyāso… satipaṭṭhānaṃ… sammappadhānaṃ tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttaṃ; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

249. Despair… the foundations of mindfulness… right striving are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and with some aspects of one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

250. Iddhipādo [V 0.0048] dvīhi khandhehi sampayutto; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayutto. Katihi vippayutto? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

250. A base of psychic power is associated with two aggregates; and with some aspects of one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

251. Jhānaṃ [M 0.0047] dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttaṃ; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi [P 0.0055] soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

251. Jhāna is associated with two aggregates, one base, and one element; and with some aspects of one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

252. Appamaññā… pañcindriyāni… pañca balāni… satta bojjhaṅgā… ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayutto; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayutto. Katihi vippayutto? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

252. The immeasurables... the five faculties... the five powers... the seven factors of enlightenment... the noble eightfold path are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and with some aspects of one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and from some aspects of one base and one element.

253. Phasso [T 0.0059]… cetanā… manasikāro tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayutto; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena [V 0.0049] ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayutto. Katihi vippayutto? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

253. Contact... volition... attention are associated with three aggregates, one sense-base, and seven elements; with one aggregate, one sense-base, and one element, they are associated in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and ten elements; from one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

254. Vedanā… saññā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

254. Feeling... perception are associated with three aggregates, one sense-base, and seven elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are associated in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and ten elements; from one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

255. Cittaṃ tīhi khandhehi sampayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttaṃ. Katihi vippayuttaṃ? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttaṃ; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttaṃ.

255. Consciousness is associated with three aggregates; with one sense-base and one element, it is associated in some respects. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and ten elements; from one sense-base and one element, it is dissociated in some respects.

256. Adhimokkho tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena dvīhi dhātūhi sampayutto; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayutto. Katihi vippayutto? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayutto; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayutto.

256. Decision is associated with three aggregates, one sense-base, and two elements; with one aggregate, one sense-base, and one element, it is associated in some respects. With how many is it dissociated? It is dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and fifteen elements; from one sense-base and one element, it is dissociated in some respects.

5. Tikaṃ

5. The Triad

257. Kusalā [M 0.0048] dhammā… akusalā dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā[T 0.0060]? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

257. Wholesome states... unwholesome states—with how many aggregates, how many sense-bases, and how many elements are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and sixteen elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

258. Sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā… dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā[P 0.0056]; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

258. States associated with pleasant feeling... states associated with painful feeling are associated with one aggregate; with one sense-base and one element, they are associated in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and fifteen elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

259. Adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

259. States associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling are associated with one aggregate; with one sense-base and one element, they are associated in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and eleven elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

260. Vipākā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

260. Resultant states... with how many are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and ten elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

261. Vipākadhammadhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

261. States that produce results... defiled and defiling states... with how many are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and sixteen elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

262. Nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammā… anupādinnupādāniyā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti[V 0.0050]? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na [T 0.0061] kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

262. States that are neither results nor produce results... states not acquired by clinging but providing a basis for clinging... with how many are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any sense-bases; they are dissociated from five elements.

263. Anupādinnaanupādāniyā dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

263. States not acquired by clinging and not providing a basis for clinging... undefiled and non-defiling states... with how many are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any sense-bases; they are dissociated from six elements.

264. Savitakkasavicārā [M 0.0049] dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

264. States with initial thought and sustained thought are associated with one aggregate, one sense-base, and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and fifteen elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.
