265. Avitakkavicāramattā dhammā… pītisahagatā dhammā [P 0.0057] ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

265. States without initial thought, with only sustained thought... states accompanied by joy are associated with one aggregate, one sense-base, and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and sixteen elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

266. Avitakkaavicārā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā vippayuttā.

266. States without initial thought and without sustained thought... with how many are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any sense-bases; they are dissociated from one element.

267. Sukhasahagatā dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

267. States accompanied by pleasure are associated with one aggregate; with one sense-base and one element, they are associated in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and fifteen elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

268. Upekkhāsahagatā dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena [T 0.0062] ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

268. States accompanied by equanimity are associated with one aggregate; with one sense-base and one element, they are associated in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and eleven elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

269. Dassanena pahātabbā dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā… ācayagāmino dhammā… apacayagāmino [V 0.0051] dhammā… sekkhā dhammā… asekkhā dhammā… mahaggatā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

269. States to be abandoned by vision, states to be abandoned by development… states with causes to be abandoned by vision… states with causes to be abandoned by development… states that lead to rebirth… states that lead to Nibbāna… states of a trainee… states of an adept… exalted states… with how many are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense-bases, and sixteen elements; with one sense-base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

270. Appamāṇā dhammā… paṇītā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

270. Immeasurable states… superior states… with how many are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any sense-bases; they are dissociated from six elements.

271. Parittārammaṇā [M 0.0050] dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

271. States that take a limited object… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

272. Mahaggatārammaṇā dhammā… appamāṇārammaṇā dhammā… hīnā dhammā… micchattaniyatā dhammā… sammattaniyatā dhammā… maggārammaṇā dhammā… maggahetukā [P 0.0058] dhammā… maggādhipatino dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā [T 0.0063] kehici vippayuttā.

272. States that take an exalted object… states that take an immeasurable object… inferior states… states fixed in wrongness… states fixed in rightness… states that take the path as object… states that have the path as a cause… states that have the path as a dominant condition… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

273. Anuppannā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

273. Unarisen states… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any bases; they are dissociated from five elements.

274. Atītārammaṇā dhammā…pe… anāgatārammaṇā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

274. States that take a past object… states that take a future object… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

275. Paccuppannārammaṇā dhammā… ajjhattārammaṇā dhammā… bahiddhārammaṇā dhammā… ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

275. States that take a present object… states that take an internal object… states that take an external object… states that take both an internal and an external object… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

276. Sanidassanasappaṭighā dhammā… anidassanasappaṭighā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti[V 0.0052]? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

276. States that are visible and impingible… states that are invisible and impingible… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

6. Dukaṃ

6. The Dyads

277. Hetū dhammā… hetū ceva sahetukā ca dhammā… hetū ceva hetusampayuttā ca dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici [T 0.0064] sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi [M 0.0051] dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

277. States that are roots… states that are roots and are with roots… states that are roots and are associated with roots are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

278. Sahetukā dhammā… hetusampayuttā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

278. States that are with roots… states associated with roots… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

279. Sahetukā ceva na ca hetū dhammā… hetusampayuttā ceva na ca hetū dhammā… na hetusahetukā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena [P 0.0059] khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

279. States that are with roots but are not roots… states that are associated with roots but are not roots… states that are not roots but are with roots are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

280. Appaccayā dhammā… asaṅkhatā dhammā… sanidassanā dhammā… sappaṭighā dhammā… rūpino dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

280. States without conditions… unconditioned states… visible states… impingible states… material states… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

281. Lokuttarā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

281. Supramundane states… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any bases; they are dissociated from six elements.

282. Āsavā [T 0.0065] dhammā… āsavā ceva sāsavā ca dhammā… āsavā ceva āsavasampayuttā ca dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena [V 0.0053] ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

282. States that are taints… states that are taints and are subject to taints… states that are taints and are associated with taints are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

283. Anāsavā dhammā… āsavavippayuttā anāsavā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

283. Taintless states… taintless states dissociated from the taints… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any bases; they are dissociated from six elements.

284. Āsavasampayuttā [M 0.0052] dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

284. States associated with the taints… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

285. Āsavasampayuttā ceva no ca āsavā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

285. States that are associated with taints but are not taints are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

286. Saṃyojanā dhammā… ganthā dhammā… oghā dhammā… yogā dhammā… nīvaraṇā dhammā… parāmāsā dhammā… parāmāsā ceva parāmaṭṭhā ca dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā [T 0.0066] kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena [P 0.0060] khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

286. States that are fetters… states that are bonds… states that are floods… states that are yokes… states that are hindrances… states that are graspings… states that are both graspings and grasped at are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; with one base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

287. Aparāmaṭṭhā dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttā aparāmaṭṭhā dhammā sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

287. States not grasped at… states not grasped at that are dissociated from grasping… with how many are they associated? There are none. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any bases; they are dissociated from six elements.

288. Parāmāsasampayuttā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

288. States associated with grasping are associated with one aggregate, one base, and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

289. Sārammaṇā [V 0.0054] dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

289. States that are with an object... are they associated? They are not. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

290. Anārammaṇā dhammā… cittavippayuttā dhammā… cittavisaṃsaṭṭhā dhammā… upādā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

290. States that are without an object... states dissociated from mind... states disjoined from mind... derived states... are they associated? They are not. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

291. Cittā [M 0.0053] dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya [T 0.0067] dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

291. States that are consciousness are associated with three aggregates; and are associated with one base and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

292. Cetasikā dhammā… cittasampayuttā dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhā dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

292. Mental factors... states associated with consciousness... states conjoined with consciousness... states conjoined with and originated by consciousness... states co-existent with those conjoined with and originated by consciousness... states that follow in sequence those conjoined with and originated by consciousness are associated with one aggregate, one base, and seven elements. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

293. Anupādinnā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

293. Unappropriated states... are they associated? They are not. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates and not from any bases, but are dissociated from five elements.

294. Upādānā [P 0.0061] dhammā… kilesā dhammā… kilesā ceva saṃkilesikā ca dhammā… kilesā ceva saṃkiliṭṭhā ca dhammā… kilesā ceva kilesasampayuttā ca dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

294. States that are clingings... states that are defilements... states that are both defilements and defiling... states that are both defilements and defiled... states that are both defilements and associated with defilements are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated with one aggregate, one base, and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

295. Asaṃkilesikā dhammā… kilesavippayuttā asaṃkilesikā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti[V 0.0055]? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

295. Non-defiling states... non-defiling states dissociated from defilements... are they associated? They are not. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates and not from any bases, but are dissociated from six elements.

296. Saṃkiliṭṭhā [T 0.0068] dhammā… kilesasampayuttā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

296. Defiled states... states associated with defilements... are they associated? They are not. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

297. Saṃkiliṭṭhā ceva no ca kilesā dhammā… kilesasampayuttā ceva no ca kilesā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā [M 0.0054] kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

297. States that are defiled but are not defilements... states that are associated with defilements but are not defilements are associated with one aggregate, one base, and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

298. Dassanena pahātabbā dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

298. States to be abandoned by vision... states to be abandoned by cultivation... states with roots to be abandoned by vision... states with roots to be abandoned by cultivation... are they associated? They are not. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

299. Savitakkā dhammā… savicārā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

299. States with initial application... states with sustained application are associated with one aggregate, one base, and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

300. Avitakkā dhammā… avicārā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya [T 0.0069] dhātuyā vippayuttā.

300. States without initial application... states without sustained application... are they associated? They are not. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates and not from any bases, but are dissociated from one element.

301. Sappītikā [P 0.0062] dhammā… pītisahagatā dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

301. States with rapture... states accompanied by rapture are associated with one aggregate, one base, and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

302. Sukhasahagatā [V 0.0056] dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

302. States accompanied by pleasure are associated with one aggregate; and are associated with one base and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

303. Upekkhāsahagatā dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

303. States accompanied by equanimity are associated with one aggregate; and are associated with one base and one element in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and eleven elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

304. Na [M 0.0055] kāmāvacarā dhammā… apariyāpannā dhammā… anuttarā dhammā…pe… sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

304. States not pertaining to the sense-sphere... unincluded states... unsurpassed states... are they associated? They are not. With how many are they dissociated? They are not dissociated from any aggregates and not from any bases, but are dissociated from six elements.

305. Rūpāvacarā dhammā… arūpāvacarā dhammā… niyyānikā dhammā… niyatā dhammā… saraṇā dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya [T 0.0070] dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

305. Phenomena of the form sphere... phenomena of the formless sphere... phenomena leading to liberation... determinate phenomena... phenomena with taints—with how many aggregates, bases, and elements are they associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are partially dissociated from one base and one element.

Dhammāyatanaṃ dhammadhātu, dukkhasaccañca jīvitaṃ;

Saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, cattāro ca mahābhavā.

The mind-object base, the mind-object element, the truth of suffering, and the life faculty; the six bases, name-and-form, and the four great kinds of existence.

Jāti jarā ca maraṇaṃ, tikesvekūnavīsati;

Gocchakesu ca paññāsa, aṭṭha cūḷantare padā.

Birth, aging, and death; nineteen in the triads, fifty in the clusters, and eight terms in the minor interval section.

Mahantare pannarasa, aṭṭhārasa tato pare;

Tevīsa padasataṃ etaṃ, sampayoge na labbhatīti.

Fifteen in the great interval, eighteen thereafter; this is one hundred and twenty-three terms that are not found in association.

Sampayogavippayogapadaniddeso chaṭṭho.

The sixth is the Exposition of the Terms of Association and Dissociation.

7. Sattamanayo

7. The Seventh Method

7. Sampayuttenavippayuttapadaniddeso

7. Exposition of Terms Dissociated from the Associated

306. Vedanākkhandhena [P 0.0063][M 0.0056][V 0.0057] ye dhammā… saññākkhandhena ye dhammā… saṅkhārakkhandhena ye dhammā… viññāṇakkhandhena ye dhammā… manāyatanena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi vippayuttā? Te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

306. With respect to phenomena associated with the feeling aggregate… with the perception aggregate… with the formations aggregate… with the consciousness aggregate… with the mind-base; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena—with how many aggregates, bases, and elements are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are partially dissociated from one base and one element.

307. Cakkhuviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā…pe… manodhātuyā ye dhammā… manoviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā vippayuttā.

307. With respect to phenomena associated with the eye-consciousness element… (and so on)… with the mind-element… with the mind-consciousness element; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… those phenomena are dissociated from no aggregates, no bases, and one element.

308. Manindriyena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

308. With respect to phenomena associated with the mind faculty; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… they are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are partially dissociated from one base and one element.

309. Upekkhindriyena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye [T 0.0071] dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

309. With respect to phenomena associated with the faculty of equanimity; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… those phenomena are dissociated from no aggregates, no bases, and five elements.

310. Saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇena ye dhammā… saḷāyatanapaccayā phassena ye dhammā… phassapaccayā [V 0.0058] vedanāya ye dhammā… phassena ye dhammā… vedanāya ye dhammā… saññāya ye dhammā… cetanāya ye dhammā… cittena ye dhammā… manasikārena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi [P 0.0064] khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

310. With respect to phenomena associated with consciousness conditioned by formations… with contact conditioned by the six bases… with feeling conditioned by contact… with contact… with feeling… with perception… with volition… with mind… with attention; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… they are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are partially dissociated from one base and one element.

311. Adhimokkhena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā vippayuttā.

311. With respect to phenomena associated with determination; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… those phenomena are dissociated from no aggregates, no bases, and one element.

312. Adukkhamasukhāya [M 0.0057] vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

312. With respect to phenomena associated with states associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling… with states accompanied by equanimity; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… those phenomena are dissociated from no aggregates, no bases, and five elements.

313. Savitakkasavicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā vippayuttā.

313. With respect to phenomena associated with states accompanied by applied thought and sustained thought; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… those phenomena are dissociated from no aggregates, no bases, and one element.

314. Cittehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cetasikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā [T 0.0072]… cittasaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

314. With respect to phenomena associated with states that are mind… with mental factors… with states associated with mind… with states mingled with mind… with states originated by what is mingled with mind… with states co-arisen with what is originated by what is mingled with mind… with states conforming to what is originated by what is mingled with mind; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… they are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are partially dissociated from one base and one element.

315. Savitakkehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā vippayuttā.

315. With respect to phenomena associated with states accompanied by applied thought… with states accompanied by sustained thought; the phenomena dissociated from these phenomena… those phenomena are dissociated from no aggregates, no bases, and one element.

316. Upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi vippayuttā? Te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

316. With respect to phenomena associated with states accompanied by equanimity; the phenomena dissociated from them—with how many aggregates, bases, and elements are they dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from no aggregates and no bases, but from five elements.

Khandhā [P 0.0065][V 0.0059] caturo āyatanañca mekaṃ;

Dhātūsu satta dvepi ca indriyato.

Four aggregates and one base; in the elements, seven, and two from the faculties.

Tayo paṭicca tathariva phassapañcamā;

Adhimuccanā manasi tikesu tīṇi.

Three from dependent origination, and likewise the five beginning with contact; determination, attention, and three from the triads.

Sattantarā dve ca manena yuttā;

Vitakkavicāraṇā upekkhakāya cāti.

Seven from the intermediate section, two associated with mind—applied and sustained thought—and the equanimity group.

Sampayuttenavippayuttapadaniddeso sattamo.

The Seventh Exposition of Terms: Dissociated from the Associated.

8. Aṭṭhamanayo

8. The Eighth Method

8. Vippayuttenasampayuttapadaniddeso

8. Exposition of Terms Associated with the Dissociated

317. Rūpakkhandhena [T 0.0073][P 0.0066][M 0.0058][V 0.0060] ye dhammā vippayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi.

317. Those phenomena that are dissociated from the form aggregate—with how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena associated? There are none.

318. Vedanākkhandhena ye dhammā… saññākkhandhena ye dhammā… saṅkhārakkhandhena ye dhammā… viññāṇakkhandhena ye dhammā…pe… saraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… araṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi.

318. Those phenomena that are dissociated from the feeling aggregate… the perception aggregate… the formations aggregate… the consciousness aggregate… those phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena with defilements… those phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena without defilements—with how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena associated? There are none.

Dhammāyatanaṃ dhammadhātu, atha jīvitaṃ nāmarūpaṃ;

Saḷāyatanaṃ jātijarāmataṃ, dve ca tike na labbhare.

The dhamma-base and the dhamma-element, then the life faculty and name-and-form; the six bases, birth, aging, and death; and two triads are not found.

Paṭhamantare satta ca, gocchake dasa aparante;

Cuddasa cha ca matthake, iccete sattacattālīsa dhammā;

Samucchede na labbhanti, moghapucchakena cāti.

Seven in the first interval, and ten in the final cluster; fourteen and six at the head—thus these forty-seven phenomena are not found in the complete analysis, and by the futile question.

Vippayuttenasampayuttapadaniddeso aṭṭhamo.

The Eighth: Exposition of Terms Associated with the Dissociated.

9. Navamanayo

9. The Ninth Method

9. Sampayuttenasampayuttapadaniddeso

9. Exposition of Terms Associated with the Associated

319. Vedanākkhandhena [P 0.0067][M 0.0059][V 0.0061] ye dhammā… saññākkhandhena ye dhammā… saṅkhārakkhandhena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttā? Te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

319. Those phenomena that are associated with the feeling aggregate… the perception aggregate… the formations aggregate, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena—with how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena associated? Those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

320. Viññāṇakkhandhena ye dhammā… manāyatanena ye dhammā… cakkhuviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā…pe… manodhātuyā ye dhammā… manoviññāṇadhātuyā [T 0.0074] ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā…pe… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

320. Those phenomena that are associated with the consciousness aggregate… the mind-base… the eye-consciousness element… the mind-element… the mind-consciousness element, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

321. Samudayasaccena ye dhammā… maggasaccena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

321. Those phenomena that are associated with the truth of origin… the truth of the path, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element.

322. Manindriyena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

322. Those phenomena that are associated with the mind faculty, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

323. Sukhindriyena ye dhammā… dukkhindriyena ye dhammā… somanassindriyena ye dhammā [V 0.0062] … domanassindriyena [P 0.0068] ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

323. Those phenomena that are associated with the pleasure faculty… the pain faculty… the joy faculty… the displeasure faculty, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

324. Upekkhindriyena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena chahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

324. Those phenomena that are associated with the faculty of equanimity, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and six elements; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

325. Saddhindriyena [M 0.0060] ye dhammā… vīriyindriyena ye dhammā… satindriyena [T 0.0075] ye dhammā… samādhindriyena ye dhammā… paññindriyena ye dhammā… anaññātaññassāmītindriyena ye dhammā… aññindriyena ye dhammā… aññātāvindriyena ye dhammā… avijjāya ye dhammā… avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

325. Those phenomena that are associated with the faith faculty… the energy faculty… the mindfulness faculty… the concentration faculty… the wisdom faculty… the faculty of ‘I shall come to know the unknown’… the faculty of final knowledge… the faculty of one who has final knowledge… with ignorance… with formations conditioned by ignorance, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element.

326. Saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

326. Those phenomena that are associated with consciousness conditioned by formations, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

327. Saḷāyatanapaccayā phassena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

327. Those phenomena that are associated with contact conditioned by the six bases, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element.

328. Phassapaccayā vedanāya ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

328. Those phenomena that are associated with feeling conditioned by contact, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

329. Vedanāpaccayā taṇhāya ye dhammā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānena ye dhammā… kammabhavena [V 0.0063] ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi [T 0.0076] ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi [P 0.0069] khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

329. Those phenomena that are associated with craving conditioned by feeling… with clinging conditioned by craving… with kamma-becoming, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element.

330. Sokena ye dhammā… dukkhena ye dhammā… domanassena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

330. Those phenomena that are associated with sorrow… with pain… with displeasure, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

331. Upāyāsena [M 0.0061] ye dhammā… satipaṭṭhānena ye dhammā… sammappadhānena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

331. Those phenomena that are associated with despair… with the establishments of mindfulness… with the right strivings, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element.

332. Iddhipādena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

332. Those phenomena that are associated with the basis of spiritual power, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with two aggregates; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element.

333. Jhānena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā dvīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

333. Those phenomena that are associated with jhāna, and those phenomena that are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with two aggregates, one base, and one element; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element.

334. Appamaññāya ye dhammā… pañcahi indriyehi ye dhammā [T 0.0077]… pañcahi balehi ye dhammā… sattahi bojjhaṅgehi ye dhammā… ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

334. What phenomena are associated with the illimitables… with the five faculties… with the five powers… with the seven factors of enlightenment… with the noble eightfold path, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; they are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

335. Phassena ye dhammā… cetanāya ye dhammā… manasikārena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

335. What phenomena are associated with contact… with volition… with attention, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; they are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

336. Vedanāya [V 0.0064] ye dhammā… saññāya ye dhammā sampayuttā[P 0.0070], tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

336. What phenomena are associated with feeling… with perception, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; they are associated with one base, one element, and with some.

337. Cittena ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

337. What phenomena are associated with mind, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates; they are associated with one base, one element, and with some.

338. Adhimokkhena [M 0.0062] ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena dvīhi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

338. What phenomena are associated with decision, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and two elements; they are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

339. Sukhāya [T 0.0078] vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

339. What phenomena are associated with phenomena associated with pleasant feeling… with phenomena associated with painful feeling… with phenomena associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with one aggregate; they are associated with one base, one element, and with some.

340. Savitakkasavicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā… avitakkavicāramattehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

340. What phenomena are associated with phenomena accompanied by applied and sustained thought… with phenomena accompanied by only sustained thought… with phenomena accompanied by joy, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

341. Sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

341. What phenomena are associated with phenomena accompanied by pleasure… with phenomena accompanied by equanimity, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with one aggregate; they are associated with one base, one element, and with some.

342. Hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva sahetukehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva hetusampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

342. What phenomena are associated with phenomena that are roots… with phenomena that are both roots and have roots… with phenomena that are both roots and are associated with roots, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; they are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

343. Sahetukehi [P 0.0071][V 0.0065] ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… na hetusahetukehi [T 0.0079] dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi na hetūhi sahetukehi (bahūsu) ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

343. What phenomena are associated with phenomena that have roots but are not roots… with phenomena that are associated with roots but are not roots… with phenomena that are not roots associated with roots, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

344. Āsavehi [M 0.0063] dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva sāsavehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva āsavasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

344. What phenomena are associated with phenomena that are taints… with phenomena that are both taints and are subject to taints… with phenomena that are both taints and are associated with taints, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; they are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

345. Āsavasampayuttehi ceva no ca āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

345. What phenomena are associated with phenomena that are associated with taints but are not taints, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

346. Saṃyojanehi… ganthehi… oghehi… yogehi… nīvaraṇehi… parāmāsehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi ceva parāmaṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

346. What phenomena are associated with phenomena that are fetters… bonds… floods… yokes… hindrances… graspings… with phenomena that are both graspings and are grasped, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; they are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

347. Parāmāsasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

347. What phenomena are associated with phenomena associated with graspings, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with one aggregate, one base, one element, and with some.

348. Cittehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye [T 0.0080] dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

348. What phenomena are associated with phenomena that are consciousness, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with three aggregates; they are associated with one base, one element, and with some.

349. Cetasikehi [P 0.0072] dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā[V 0.0066], tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā.

349. What phenomena are associated with phenomena that are mental factors… with phenomena associated with consciousness… with phenomena mingled with consciousness… with phenomena originated from what is mingled with consciousness, and what phenomena are associated with those phenomena… those phenomena are associated with one aggregate, one base, and seven elements.

350. Upādānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkilesikehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkiliṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva kilesasampayuttehi [M 0.0064] ca dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

350. With phenomena that are clingings... with phenomena that are defilements... with phenomena that are both defilements and subject to defilement... with phenomena that are both defilements and defiled... with phenomena that are both defilements and associated with defilements; whatever phenomena are associated with these, and whatever phenomena are in turn associated with those phenomena—those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and one element; they are associated with one aggregate, one base, and one element in some cases.

351. Saṃkiliṭṭhehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savitakkehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sappītikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena [T 0.0081] ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

351. With phenomena that are defiled but are not defilements... with phenomena that are associated with defilements but are not defilements... with phenomena that are accompanied by initial application... with phenomena that are accompanied by sustained application... with phenomena that are accompanied by rapture... with phenomena that are accompanied by rapture; whatever phenomena are associated with these, and whatever phenomena are in turn associated with those phenomena—those phenomena are associated with one aggregate, one base, and one element in some cases.

352. Sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttā? Te dhammā ekena khandhena sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā.

352. With phenomena that are accompanied by pleasure... with phenomena that are accompanied by equanimity; whatever phenomena are associated with these, and whatever phenomena are in turn associated with those phenomena, with how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are those phenomena associated? Those phenomena are associated with one aggregate; they are associated with one base and one element in some cases.

Arūpakkhandhā cattāro, manāyatanameva ca;

Viññāṇadhātuyo satta, dve saccā cuddasindriyā.

The four immaterial aggregates, and the mind base itself; seven consciousness elements, two truths, and fourteen faculties.

Paccaye dvādasa padā, tato upari soḷasa;

Tikesu aṭṭha gocchake, tecattālīsameva ca.

Twelve terms in the Conditions section, sixteen above that; eight in the Triads, and forty-three in the cluster.

Mahantaraduke satta, padā piṭṭhi dukesu cha;

Navamassa padassete, niddese saṅgahaṃ gatāti.

Seven terms in the Great Intermediate Couplets, six in the Back Couplets; these are included in the exposition of the ninth term.

Sampayuttenasampayuttapadaniddeso navamo.

The Ninth is the Exposition of the Term 'Associated'.

10. Dasamanayo

10. The Tenth Manner

10. Vippayuttenavippayuttapadaniddeso

10. Exposition of the Term 'Dissociated from the Dissociated'

353. Rūpakkhandhena [P 0.0073][M 0.0065][V 0.0067] ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi vippayuttā? Te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

Whatever phenomena are dissociated from the form aggregate, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena—with how many aggregates, bases, and elements are they dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

354. Vedanākkhandhena ye dhammā… saññākkhandhena ye dhammā… saṅkhārakkhandhena ye dhammā… viññāṇakkhandhena ye dhammā… manāyatanena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā…pe… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

With phenomena that are dissociated from the feeling aggregate... from the perception aggregate... from the formations aggregate... from the consciousness aggregate... from the mind-base; whatever phenomena are dissociated from these, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

355. Cakkhāyatanena [T 0.0082] ye dhammā…pe… phoṭṭhabbāyatanena ye dhammā… cakkhudhātuyā ye dhammā…pe… phoṭṭhabbadhātuyā ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

With phenomena that are dissociated from the eye-base... from the tangible-object-base... from the eye-element... from the tangible-object-element; whatever phenomena are dissociated from these, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

356. Cakkhuviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā…pe… manoviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… samudayasaccena ye dhammā… maggasaccena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

10. With phenomena that are dissociated from the eye-consciousness-element... from the mind-consciousness-element... from the truth of the origin... from the truth of the path; whatever phenomena are dissociated from these, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

357. Nirodhasaccena [V 0.0068] ye dhammā… cakkhundriyena ye dhammā…pe… kāyindriyena ye dhammā.. itthindriyena [P 0.0074] ye dhammā… purisindriyena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

10. With phenomena that are dissociated from the truth of cessation... from the eye faculty... from the body faculty... from the femininity faculty... from the masculinity faculty; whatever phenomena are dissociated from these, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

358. Manindriyena [M 0.0066] ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

353. Whatever phenomena are dissociated from the mind faculty, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

359. Sukhindriyena ye dhammā… dukkhindriyena ye dhammā… somanassindriyena ye dhammā… domanassindriyena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi [T 0.0083] dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

354. With phenomena that are dissociated from the happiness faculty... from the pain faculty... from the gladness faculty... from the sadness faculty; whatever phenomena are dissociated from these, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

360. Upekkhindriyena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

355. Whatever phenomena are dissociated from the equanimity faculty, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and eleven elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

361. Saddhindriyena ye dhammā… vīriyindriyena ye dhammā… satindriyena ye dhammā… samādhindriyena ye dhammā… paññindriyena ye dhammā… anaññātaññassāmītindriyena ye dhammā… aññindriyena ye dhammā… aññātāvindriyena ye dhammā… avijjāya ye dhammā… avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

356. With phenomena that are dissociated from the faith faculty... from the energy faculty... from the mindfulness faculty... from the concentration faculty... from the wisdom faculty... from the faculty of ‘I shall know the unknown’... from the faculty of final knowledge... from the faculty of one who has final knowledge... from ignorance... from formations conditioned by ignorance; whatever phenomena are dissociated from these, and whatever phenomena are in turn dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated in part from one base and one element.

362. Saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇena ye dhammā… saḷāyatanapaccayā phassena ye dhammā… phassapaccayā vedanāya ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena [V 0.0069] khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

362. Phenomena that are dissociated from consciousness conditioned by formations... from contact conditioned by the sixfold base... from feeling conditioned by contact; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

363. Vedanāpaccayā taṇhāya ye dhammā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānena [T 0.0084] ye dhammā… kammabhavena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena [P 0.0075] khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

363. Phenomena that are dissociated from craving conditioned by feeling... from clinging conditioned by craving... from kamma-becoming; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

364. Rūpabhavena [M 0.0067] ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

364. Phenomena that are dissociated from form-becoming; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates nor from any bases, but are dissociated from three elements.

365. Asaññābhavena ye dhammā… ekavokārabhavena ye dhammā… paridevena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

365. Phenomena that are dissociated from non-percipient existence... from one-constituent existence... from lamentation; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

366. Arūpabhavena ye dhammā… nevasaññānāsaññābhavena ye dhammā… catuvokārabhavena ye dhammā… sokena ye dhammā… dukkhena ye dhammā… domanassena ye dhammā… upāyāsena ye dhammā… satipaṭṭhānena ye dhammā… sammappadhānena ye dhammā… iddhipādena ye dhammā… jhānena ye dhammā… appamaññāya ye dhammā… pañcahi indriyehi ye dhammā… pañcahi balehi ye dhammā… sattahi bojjhaṅgehi ye dhammā… ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena [T 0.0085] ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

366. Phenomena that are dissociated from formless existence... from neither-perception-nor-non-perception existence... from four-constituent existence... from sorrow... from pain... from displeasure... from despair... from the foundations of mindfulness... from the right exertions... from the bases of spiritual power... from jhāna... from the illimitables... from the five faculties... from the five powers... from the seven factors of enlightenment... from the noble eight-factored path; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

367. Phassena ye dhammā… vedanāya ye dhammā… saññāya ye dhammā… cetanāya ye dhammā… cittena ye dhammā… manasikārena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

367. Phenomena that are dissociated from contact... from feeling... from perception... from volition... from consciousness... from attention; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

368. Adhimokkhena ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā [V 0.0070] ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

368. Phenomena that are dissociated from decision; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

1. Tikaṃ

1. The Triad

369. Kusalehi dhammehi ye dhammā… akusalehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena [P 0.0076] khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

1. Phenomena that are dissociated from wholesome phenomena... from unwholesome phenomena; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

370. Sukhāya [M 0.0068] vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

369. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena associated with pleasant feeling... from phenomena associated with painful feeling; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

371. Adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā[T 0.0086], tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

370. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and eleven elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

372. Vipākehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

371. Phenomena that are dissociated from resultant phenomena; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

373. Vipākadhammadhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

372. Phenomena that are dissociated from result-producing phenomena... from phenomena that are defiled and are a condition for defilements; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

374. Nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammehi ye dhammā… anupādinnupādāniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

373. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena that are neither resultant nor result-producing... from phenomena that are not taken up but are fuel for clinging; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates nor from any bases, but are dissociated from five elements.

375. Anupādinnaanupādāniyehi [V 0.0071] dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

374. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena that are not taken up and are not fuel for clinging... from phenomena that are undefiled and not a condition for defilements; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates nor from any bases, but are dissociated from six elements.

376. Savitakkasavicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi [T 0.0087] ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena [P 0.0077] khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

375. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena accompanied by applied and sustained thought; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

377. Avitakkavicāramattehi [M 0.0069] dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

376. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena without applied thought but with only sustained thought... from phenomena accompanied by rapture; phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

378. Avitakkaavicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā vippayuttā.

378. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena without applied thought and sustained thought; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any bases, but are dissociated from one element.

379. Sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

379. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena accompanied by pleasure; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

380. Upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

380. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena accompanied by equanimity; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and eleven elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

381. Dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi [T 0.0088] ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ācayagāmīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… apacayagāmīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… sekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… mahaggatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi [V 0.0072] dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

381. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena to be abandoned by vision... from phenomena to be abandoned by development... from phenomena with causes to be abandoned by vision... from phenomena with causes to be abandoned by development... from phenomena that lead to accumulation... from phenomena that lead to non-accumulation... from phenomena of a trainee... from phenomena of one beyond training... from exalted phenomena; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

382. Appamāṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… paṇītehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na [P 0.0078] kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

382. Phenomena that are dissociated from immeasurable phenomena... from sublime phenomena; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any bases, but are dissociated from six elements.

383. Parittārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi [M 0.0070] dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

383. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena with limited objects; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

384. Mahaggatārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… appamāṇārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hīnehi dhammehi ye dhammā… micchattaniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sammattaniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggādhipatīhi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

384. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena with exalted objects... from phenomena with immeasurable objects... from inferior phenomena... from phenomena fixed in wrongness... from phenomena fixed in rightness... from phenomena with the path as object... from phenomena with the path as cause... from phenomena with the path as dominant influence; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

385. Anuppannehi [T 0.0089] dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

385. Phenomena that are dissociated from unarisen phenomena; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any bases, but are dissociated from five elements.

386. Atītārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anāgatārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

386. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena with past objects... from phenomena with future objects; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

387. Paccuppannārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bahiddhārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

387. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena with present objects... from phenomena with internal objects... from phenomena with external objects... from phenomena with internal-and-external objects; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

388. Sanidassanasappaṭighehi [V 0.0073] dhammehi ye dhammā… anidassanasappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā[P 0.0079], tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

388. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena with manifestation and with resistance... from phenomena without manifestation but with resistance; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

2. Dukaṃ

2. The Dyad

389. Hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… sahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva sahetukehi ca dhammehi [M 0.0071] ye dhammā… sahetukehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye [T 0.0090] dhammā… hetūhi ceva hetusampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… na hetusahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

389. Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena that are roots... from phenomena with roots... from phenomena associated with roots... from phenomena that are both roots and with roots... from phenomena that are with roots but are not roots... from phenomena that are both roots and associated with roots... from phenomena that are associated with roots but are not roots... from phenomena that are neither roots nor with roots; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

390. Appaccayehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṅkhatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sanidassanehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā… rūpīhi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

390.
