Phenomena that are dissociated from phenomena without conditions... from unconditioned phenomena... from phenomena with manifestation... from phenomena with resistance... from material phenomena; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

391. Lokuttarehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

391. Phenomena that are dissociated from supramundane phenomena; and phenomena that are dissociated from those phenomena... those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates, nor from any bases, but are dissociated from six elements.

392. Āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva sāsavehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva āsavasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavasampayuttehi ceva no ca āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

392. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are taints... dissociated from phenomena associated with taints... dissociated from phenomena that are both taints and objects of taints... dissociated from phenomena that are both taints and associated with taints... and dissociated from phenomena that are associated with taints but are not taints: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

393. Anāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavavippayuttehi anāsavehi [T 0.0091] dhammehi ye dhammā [V 0.0074] vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na [P 0.0080] kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

393. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are not objects of the taints... and dissociated from phenomena that are dissociated from taints and are not objects of the taints: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates or any bases, but are dissociated from six elements.

394. Saṃyojanehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ganthehi dhammehi ye dhammā… oghehi dhammehi ye dhammā… yogehi dhammehi ye dhammā… nīvaraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsasampayuttehi dhammehi [M 0.0072] ye dhammā… parāmāsehi ceva parāmaṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

394. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are fetters... dissociated from phenomena that are bonds... dissociated from phenomena that are floods... dissociated from phenomena that are yokes... dissociated from phenomena that are hindrances... dissociated from phenomena that are graspings... dissociated from phenomena associated with graspings... and dissociated from phenomena that are both graspings and objects of grasping: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

395. Aparāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttehi aparāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

395. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are not objects of grasping... and dissociated from phenomena that are dissociated from graspings and are not objects of grasping: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates or any bases, but are dissociated from six elements.

396. Sārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cetasikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi [T 0.0092] dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

396. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena with an object... dissociated from consciousness... dissociated from mental factors... dissociated from phenomena associated with consciousness... dissociated from phenomena conjoined with consciousness... dissociated from phenomena originated from that which is conjoined with consciousness... dissociated from phenomena co-existent with that which is originated from that which is conjoined with consciousness... and dissociated from phenomena that follow upon that which is originated from that which is conjoined with consciousness: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

397. Anārammaṇehi anupādiṇṇehi (sī. ka.) dhammehi ye dhammā… cittavippayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittavisaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādādhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

397. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena without an object... dissociated from phenomena dissociated from consciousness... dissociated from phenomena not conjoined with consciousness... and dissociated from phenomena that have arisen in dependence: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

398. Anupādinnehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na [P 0.0081] kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

398. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena not grasped by kamma as its result: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates or any bases, but are dissociated from five elements.

399. Upādānehi [V 0.0075] dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkilesikehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkiliṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhehi ceva no [M 0.0073] ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva kilesasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

399. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are clingings... dissociated from phenomena that are defilements... dissociated from defiled phenomena... dissociated from phenomena associated with defilements... dissociated from phenomena that are both defilements and a basis for defilements... dissociated from phenomena that are both defilements and defiled... dissociated from phenomena that are defiled but are not defilements... dissociated from phenomena that are both defilements and associated with defilements... and dissociated from phenomena that are associated with defilements but are not defilements: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

400. Asaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesavippayuttehi asaṃkilesikehi [T 0.0093] dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

400. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are not a basis for defilements... and dissociated from phenomena that are dissociated from defilements and are not a basis for defilements: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates or any bases, but are dissociated from six elements.

401. Dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

401. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena to be abandoned by vision... dissociated from phenomena to be abandoned by development... dissociated from phenomena with roots to be abandoned by vision... and dissociated from phenomena with roots to be abandoned by development: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

402. Savitakkehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

402. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena with applied thought... and dissociated from phenomena with sustained thought: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

403. Avitakkehi dhammehi ye dhammā… avicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā vippayuttā.

403. With regard to phenomena dissociated from phenomena without applied thought... and dissociated from phenomena without sustained thought: from these phenomena, which phenomena are dissociated? Those phenomena are not dissociated from any aggregates or any bases, but are dissociated from one element.

404. Sappītikehi [P 0.0082] dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

404. Those dhammas that are dissociated from dhammas accompanied by rapture, and are in turn dissociated from those—they are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

405. Sukhasahagatehi [T 0.0094][V 0.0076] dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi [M 0.0074] vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

405. With regard to those dhammas that are dissociated from dhammas accompanied by pleasure—from those dhammas, which dhammas are dissociated? Those dhammas are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

406. Upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

406. With regard to those dhammas that are dissociated from dhammas accompanied by equanimity—from those dhammas, which dhammas are dissociated? Those dhammas are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and eleven elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

407. Na kāmāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… apariyāpannehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anuttarehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi vippayuttā.

407. With regard to those dhammas that are dissociated from dhammas not of the sense-sphere, those dhammas that are dissociated from unincluded dhammas, and those dhammas that are dissociated from unsurpassed dhammas—from those dhammas, which dhammas are dissociated? Those dhammas are not dissociated from any aggregates, not from any bases, and not from any elements.

408. Rūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… arūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… niyyānikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… niyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, tehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi vippayuttā? Te dhammā ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

408. With regard to those dhammas that are dissociated from dhammas of the form-sphere, those dhammas that are dissociated from dhammas of the formless-sphere, those dhammas that are dissociated from dhammas that lead to liberation, those dhammas that are dissociated from definite dhammas, and those dhammas that are dissociated from dhammas with defilements—from those dhammas, which dhammas are dissociated? From how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are those dhammas dissociated? Those dhammas are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements; they are dissociated from one base and one element in some respects.

Dhammāyatanaṃ dhammadhātu, dukkhasaccañca jīvitaṃ;

Saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, cattāro ca mahābhavā.

The dhamma-base, the dhamma-element, the truth of suffering, and life; the six sense-bases, name-and-form, and the four great primary elements.

Jāti jarā ca maraṇaṃ, tikesvekūnavīsati;

Gocchakesu ca paññāsa, aṭṭha cūḷantare padā.

Birth, aging, and death; nineteen in the triads; fifty in the clusters; and eight terms in the minor intermediate section.

Mahantare pannarasa, aṭṭhārasa tato pare;

Tevīsa padasataṃ etaṃ, sampayoge na labbhatīti.

Fifteen in the major intermediate section, eighteen thereafter; these one hundred and twenty-three terms are not found in association.

Vippayuttenavippayuttapadaniddeso dasamo.

Tenth: The Exposition of Terms Dissociated from the Dissociated.

11. Ekādasamanayo

11. The Eleventh Method

11. Saṅgahitenasampayuttavippayuttapadaniddeso

11. Exposition of Terms Included, Associated, and Dissociated

409. Samudayasaccena [T 0.0095][P 0.0083][M 0.0075][V 0.0077] ye dhammā… maggasaccena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttā? Te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

409. By the truth of the origin, which phenomena... by the truth of the path, which phenomena are included by the classification of aggregates, included by the classification of bases, and included by the classification of elements? With how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are those phenomena associated? Those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

410. Itthindriyena ye dhammā… purisindriyena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

410. By the female faculty, which phenomena... by the male faculty, which phenomena are included by the classification of aggregates, included by the classification of bases, and included by the classification of elements? With how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are those phenomena associated? None. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

411. Sukhindriyena ye dhammā… dukkhindriyena ye dhammā… somanassindriyena ye dhammā… domanassindriyena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena [T 0.0096] khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

411. By the pleasure faculty, which phenomena... by the pain faculty, which phenomena... by the joy faculty, which phenomena... by the displeasure faculty, which phenomena are included by the classification of aggregates, included by the classification of bases, and included by the classification of elements...? Those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

412. Upekkhindriyena [V 0.0078] ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā… te dhammā tīhi [P 0.0084] khandhehi ekenāyatanena dvīhi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

412. By the equanimity faculty, which phenomena are included by the classification of aggregates, included by the classification of bases, and included by the classification of elements...? Those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and two elements; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

413. Saddhindriyena [M 0.0076] ye dhammā… vīriyindriyena ye dhammā… satindriyena ye dhammā… samādhindriyena ye dhammā… paññindriyena ye dhammā… anaññātaññassāmītindriyena ye dhammā… aññindriyena ye dhammā… aññātāvindriyena ye dhammā… avijjāya ye dhammā… avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārehi ye dhammā… saḷāyatanapaccayā phassena ye dhammā… vedanāpaccayā taṇhāya ye dhammā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānena ye dhammā… kammabhavena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

413. By the faith faculty... by the energy faculty... by the mindfulness faculty... by the concentration faculty... by the wisdom faculty... by the faculty of 'I shall come to know the unknown'... by the faculty of final knowledge... by the faculty of one who has final knowledge... by ignorance... by formations conditioned by ignorance... by contact conditioned by the sixfold base... by craving conditioned by feeling... by clinging conditioned by craving... by kamma-existence: which phenomena are included by the classification of aggregates, included by the classification of bases, and included by the classification of elements? Those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

414. Paridevena [T 0.0097] ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

414. By lamentation, which phenomena are included by the classification of aggregates, included by the classification of bases, and included by the classification of elements? With how many aggregates, how many bases, and how many elements are those phenomena associated? None. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

415. Sokena ye dhammā… dukkhena ye dhammā… domanassena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

415. By sorrow, which phenomena... by pain, which phenomena... by displeasure, which phenomena are included by the aggregate classification, included by the base classification, and included by the element classification...? Those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are associated in some respects with one base and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

416. Upāyāsena ye dhammā… satipaṭṭhānena ye dhammā… sammappadhānena [P 0.0085] ye dhammā… appamaññāya ye dhammā… pañcahi indriyehi ye dhammā… pañcahi balehi ye dhammā… sattahi bojjhaṅgehi [V 0.0079] ye dhammā… ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena ye dhammā… phassena ye dhammā… cetanāya ye dhammā… adhimokkhena ye dhammā… manasikārena ye dhammā… hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva sahetukehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva hetusampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva sāsavehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva āsavasampayuttehi [M 0.0077] ca dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃyojanehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ganthehi dhammehi ye dhammā… oghehi dhammehi ye dhammā… yogehi dhammehi ye dhammā… nīvaraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkilesikehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkiliṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva kilesasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena saṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena saṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena saṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttā? Te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi sampayuttā; ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

416. By despair, which phenomena... by the foundations of mindfulness, which phenomena... by the right strivings... by the immeasurables... by the five faculties... by the five powers... by the seven enlightenment factors... by the noble eightfold path... by contact... by volition... by decision... by attention... by phenomena that are roots... by phenomena that are both roots and have roots... by phenomena that are both roots and are associated with roots... by phenomena that are taints... by phenomena that are both taints and are subject to taints... by phenomena that are both taints and are associated with taints... by phenomena that are fetters... ties... floods... yokes... hindrances... misapprehensions... clinging... defilements... by phenomena that are both defilements and are subject to defilement... by phenomena that are both defilements and are defiled... and by phenomena that are both defilements and are associated with defilements, are not included by the aggregate classification, but are included by the base classification and the element classification? With how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena associated? Those phenomena are associated with three aggregates, one base, and seven elements; and are associated in some respects with one aggregate, one base, and one element. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; and are dissociated in some respects from one base and one element.

Dve saccā pannarasindriyā, ekādasa paṭiccapadā;

Uddhaṃ puna ekādasa, gocchakapadamettha tiṃsavidhāti.

Two truths, fifteen faculties, eleven terms of dependent origination; again, eleven from above, and here the terms from the clusters are of thirty kinds.

Saṅgahitenasampayuttavippayuttapadaniddeso ekādasamo.

The Eleventh: Exposition of Included, Associated, and Dissociated Terms.

12. Dvādasamanayo

12. The Twelfth Manner

12. Sampayuttenasaṅgahitāsaṅgahitapadaniddeso

12. Exposition of the Included and Unincluded Terms by Association

417. Vedanākkhandhena [P 0.0086][M 0.0078][V 0.0080] ye dhammā… saññākkhandhena ye dhammā… saṅkhārakkhandhena ye dhammā sampayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi [T 0.0099] aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

Those phenomena associated with the aggregate of feeling... those phenomena associated with the aggregate of perception... those phenomena associated with the aggregate of formations—by how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena included? Those phenomena are included by three aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and ten elements.

418. Viññāṇakkhandhena ye dhammā… manāyatanena ye dhammā… cakkhuviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā…pe… manodhātuyā ye dhammā… manoviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā sampayuttā…pe… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

12. Those phenomena associated with the aggregate of consciousness... those phenomena associated with the mind-base... those phenomena associated with the eye-consciousness element... and so on... those phenomena associated with the mind-element... those phenomena associated with the mind-consciousness element... and so on... those phenomena are included by three aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

419. Samudayasaccena ye dhammā… maggasaccena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

12. Those phenomena associated with the truth of the origin... those phenomena associated with the truth of the path... those phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

420. Manindriyena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0087] asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

417. Those phenomena associated with the mind faculty... those phenomena are included by three aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

421. Sukhindriyena [V 0.0081] ye dhammā… dukkhindriyena ye dhammā… somanassindriyena ye dhammā… domanassindriyena ye dhammā sampayuttā…, te dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

418. Those phenomena associated with the faculty of pleasure... those phenomena associated with the faculty of pain... those phenomena associated with the faculty of gladness... those phenomena associated with the faculty of sadness... those phenomena are included by three aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

422. Upekkhindriyena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi [T 0.0100] dvīhāyatanehi sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

419. Those phenomena associated with the faculty of equanimity... those phenomena are included by three aggregates, two bases, and seven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and eleven elements.

423. Saddhindriyena [M 0.0079] ye dhammā… vīriyindriyena ye dhammā… satindriyena ye dhammā… samādhindriyena ye dhammā… paññindriyena ye dhammā… anaññātaññassāmītindriyena ye dhammā… aññindriyena ye dhammā… aññātāvindriyena ye dhammā… avijjāya ye dhammā… avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

420. Those phenomena associated with the faculty of faith... with the faculty of energy... with the faculty of mindfulness... with the faculty of concentration... with the faculty of wisdom... with the faculty of 'I will know what is not yet known'... with the faculty of final knowledge... with the faculty of one who has final knowledge... those phenomena associated with ignorance... those phenomena associated with formations conditioned by ignorance... those phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

424. Saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

424. Those phenomena associated with consciousness conditioned by formations... these phenomena are included by three aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

425. Saḷāyatanapaccayā [P 0.0088] phassena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

425. Those phenomena associated with contact conditioned by the sixfold sense base... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

426. Phassapaccayā vedanāya ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

426. Those phenomena associated with feeling conditioned by contact... these phenomena are included by three aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and ten elements.

427. Vedanāpaccayā taṇhāya ye dhammā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānena [T 0.0101] ye dhammā… kammabhavena [V 0.0082] ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

427. Those phenomena associated with craving conditioned by feeling... those phenomena associated with clinging conditioned by craving... those phenomena associated with kamma-becoming... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

428. Sokena ye dhammā… dukkhena ye dhammā… domanassena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

428. Those phenomena associated with sorrow... those phenomena associated with pain... those phenomena associated with displeasure... these phenomena are included by three aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

429. Upāyāsena ye dhammā… satipaṭṭhānena ye dhammā… sammappadhānena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi [M 0.0080] saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

429. Those phenomena associated with despair... those phenomena associated with the foundations of mindfulness... those phenomena associated with the right strivings... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

430. Iddhipādena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi [P 0.0089] khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

430. Those phenomena associated with the bases for spiritual power... these phenomena are included by three aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

431. Jhānena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

431. Those phenomena associated with jhāna... these phenomena are included by three aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

432. Appamaññāya ye dhammā… pañcahi indriyehi ye dhammā… pañcahi balehi ye dhammā… sattahi bojjhaṅgehi ye dhammā… ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te [T 0.0102] dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

432. Those phenomena associated with the immeasurables... with the five faculties... with the five powers... with the seven factors of enlightenment... with the Noble Eightfold Path... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

433. Phassena ye dhammā… cetanāya ye dhammā… manasikārena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

433. Those phenomena associated with contact... those phenomena associated with volition... those phenomena associated with attention... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

434. Vedanāya ye dhammā… saññāya ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi [V 0.0083] aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

434. Those phenomena associated with feeling... those phenomena associated with perception... these phenomena are included by three aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, ten bases, and ten elements.

435. Cittena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0090] asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

435. Those phenomena associated with consciousness... these phenomena are included by three aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

436. Adhimokkhena ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

436. Those phenomena associated with decision... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements.

437. Sukhāya [M 0.0081] vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savitakkasavicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā… avitakkavicāramattehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi [T 0.0103] ye dhammā… sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

437. Those phenomena associated with phenomena associated with pleasant feeling... those phenomena associated with phenomena associated with painful feeling... those phenomena associated with phenomena associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling... those phenomena associated with phenomena with applied and sustained thought... those phenomena associated with phenomena with sustained thought only... those phenomena associated with phenomena accompanied by rapture... those phenomena associated with phenomena accompanied by pleasure... those phenomena associated with phenomena accompanied by equanimity... these phenomena are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

438. Hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva sahetukehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva hetusampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

438. Those phenomena associated with phenomena that are roots... those phenomena associated with phenomena that are both roots and have a root... those phenomena associated with phenomena that are both roots and are associated with a root... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

439. Sahetukehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… na hetusahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

439. Those phenomena associated with phenomena that have a root but are not roots... those phenomena associated with phenomena that are associated with a root but are not roots... those phenomena associated with phenomena that are not root-with-a-root... these phenomena are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

440. Āsavehi [V 0.0084] dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva sāsavehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva āsavasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0091] asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

440. Which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are taints... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are both taints and subject to taints... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are both taints and associated with taints... those phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

441. Āsavasampayuttehi ceva no ca āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā [T 0.0104] sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

441. Which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are associated with taints but are not taints... those phenomena are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

442. Saṃyojanehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ganthehi dhammehi ye dhammā… oghehi dhammehi ye dhammā… yogehi dhammehi ye dhammā… nīvaraṇehi dhammehi [M 0.0082] ye dhammā… parāmāsehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi ceva parāmaṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

442. Which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are fetters... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are bonds... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are floods... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are yokes... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are hindrances... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are adherences... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are both adherences and are adhered to... those phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

443. Parāmāsasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

443. Which phenomena are associated with phenomena associated with adherence... those phenomena are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

444. Cittehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Dvīhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

444. Which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are consciousness... those phenomena are included by three aggregates, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by two aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

445. Cetasikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhūhi dhammehi ye [T 0.0105] dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattīhi [P 0.0092] dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

445. Which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are mental factors... which phenomena are associated with phenomena associated with consciousness... which phenomena are associated with phenomena mixed with consciousness... which phenomena are associated with phenomena originating from what is mixed with consciousness... which phenomena are associated with phenomena co-arisen with what originates from what is mixed with consciousness... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that revolve in dependence on what originates from what is mixed with consciousness... those phenomena are included by one aggregate, one base, and seven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and eleven elements.

446. Upādānehi [V 0.0085] dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkilesikehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkiliṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva kilesasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

446. Which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are clingings... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are defilements... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are both defilements and defiling... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are both defilements and defiled... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are both defilements and associated with defilements... those phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

447. Saṃkiliṭṭhehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savitakkehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sappītikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhasahagatehi dhammehi [M 0.0083] ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā sampayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā ekena khandhena ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

447. Which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are defiled but are not defilements... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that are associated with defilements but are not defilements... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that have applied thought... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that have sustained thought... which phenomena are associated with phenomena that have rapture... which phenomena are associated with phenomena accompanied by rapture... which phenomena are associated with phenomena accompanied by happiness... which phenomena are associated with phenomena accompanied by equanimity? By how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena included? They are included by one aggregate, one base, and one element. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, eleven bases, and seventeen elements.

Rūpakkhandhā cattāro, manāyatanameva ca;

Viññāṇadhātuyo satta, dve saccā cuddasindriyā.

The four form aggregates, and the mind base as well; seven consciousness elements, two truths, and fourteen faculties.

Paccaye dvādasa padā, tato upari soḷasa;

Tikesu aṭṭha gocchake, tecattālīsameva ca.

Twelve terms in Conditions, and sixteen above that; eight in the Triads, and forty-three in the Couplet-groups.

Mahantaraduke satta, padā piṭṭhidukesu cha;

Navamassa padassete, niddese saṅgahaṃ gatāti.

Seven terms in the Great Intermediate Couplets, six in the Back Couplets; these are included in the exposition of the ninth term.

Sampayuttenasaṅgahitāsaṅgahitapadaniddeso dvādasamo.

The twelfth exposition of the statement on the associated, included, and unincluded.

13. Terasamanayo

13. The Thirteenth Manner

13. Asaṅgahitenasampayuttavippayuttapadaniddeso

13. Exposition of the Statement on Terms Associated with or Dissociated from the Unincluded

448. Rūpakkhandhena [T 0.0106][P 0.0093][M 0.0084][V 0.0086] ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttā? Te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

With respect to the form aggregate: which phenomena are unincluded by aggregate inclusion, unincluded by base inclusion, and unincluded by element inclusion? With how many aggregates, bases, and elements are those phenomena associated? They are associated with three aggregates; with one base and one element, they are associated in part. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; from one base and one element, they are dissociated in part.

449. Dhammāyatanena ye dhammā… dhammadhātuyā ye dhammā… itthindriyena ye dhammā… purisindriyena ye dhammā… jīvitindriyena ye dhammā… viññāṇapaccayā nāmarūpena ye dhammā… asaññābhavena ye dhammā… ekavokārabhavena ye dhammā… jātiyā ye dhammā… jarāya ye dhammā… maraṇena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā…pe… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

With respect to the mind-object base... the mind-object element... the female faculty... the male faculty... the life faculty... name-and-form conditioned by consciousness... non-percipient existence... one-constituent existence... birth... aging... death, which phenomena are unincluded by aggregate inclusion, unincluded by base inclusion, and unincluded by element inclusion? ...pe... Those phenomena are associated with three aggregates; with one base and one element, they are associated in part. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements; from one base and one element, they are dissociated in part.

450. Arūpabhavena ye dhammā… nevasaññānāsaññābhavena ye dhammā… catuvokārabhavena ye dhammā… iddhipādena ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā[T 0.0107], te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti[V 0.0087]? Natthi? Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi [P 0.0094] khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

450. In relation to formless existence... the existence of neither-perception-nor-non-perception... four-constituent existence... the bases of psychic power: which phenomena are not included by the aggregate-count, not included by the sense-base-count, and not included by the element-count? With how many aggregates, sense bases, and elements are those phenomena associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one sense base, and seven elements; from one sense base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

451. Kusalehi dhammehi ye dhammā… akusalehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… vipākehi dhammehi ye dhammā… vipākadhammadhammehi ye dhammā… anupādinnaanupādāniyehi [M 0.0085] dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savitakkasavicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā… avitakkavicāramattehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ācayagāmīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… apacayagāmīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… sekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… mahaggatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… appamāṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parittārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… mahaggatārammaṇehi dhammehi ye [T 0.0108] dhammā… appamāṇārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hīnehi dhammehi ye dhammā… paṇītehi dhammehi ye dhammā… micchattaniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sammattaniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggādhipatīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… atītārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anāgatārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… paccuppannārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bahiddhārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sahetukehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… na hetusahetukehi [P 0.0095] dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

451. In relation to wholesome states; ...unwholesome states; ...states associated with pleasant feeling; ...painful feeling; ...neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling; ...resultant states; ...states that produce results; ...states not clung to and not objects of clinging; ...states that are defiled and are objects of defilement; ...states that are undefiled and are not objects of defilement; ...states with applied and sustained thought; ...states with sustained thought only; ...states accompanied by rapture; ...states accompanied by pleasure; ...states accompanied by equanimity; ...states to be abandoned by seeing; ...states to be abandoned by development; ...states with roots to be abandoned by seeing; ...states with roots to be abandoned by development; ...states leading to accumulation; ...states leading to de-accumulation; ...states of a trainee; ...states of one beyond training; ...exalted states; ...immeasurable states; ...states with a limited object; ...states with an exalted object; ...states with an immeasurable object; ...inferior states; ...sublime states; ...states fixed in wrongness; ...states fixed in rightness; ...states with the path as object; ...states with the path as root; ...states with the path as dominant factor; ...states with a past object; ...states with a future object; ...states with a present object; ...states with an internal object; ...states with an external object; ...states with an internal-and-external object; ...states with roots; ...states associated with roots; ...states that have roots but are not roots; ...states associated with roots but are not roots; ...states that are not roots but have a root: which phenomena are not included by the aggregate-count, not included by the sense-base-count, and not included by the element-count? With how many aggregates, sense bases, and elements are those phenomena associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one sense base, and seven elements; from one sense base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

452. Rūpīhi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena [V 0.0088] asaṅgahitā… te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi [M 0.0086] vippayuttā[T 0.0109]; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

452. In relation to material states: those phenomena which are not included by the aggregate-count, not included by the sense-base-count, and not included by the element-count... those phenomena are associated with three aggregates; with one sense base and one element, they are associated in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements; from one sense base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

453. Arūpīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… lokuttarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavavippayuttehi ceva no ca āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavavippayuttehi anāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṃyojaniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… aganthaniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anoghaniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ayoganiyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anīvaraṇiyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… aparāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttehi aparāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

453. In relation to immaterial states; ...supramundane states; ...taintless states; ...states associated with taints; ...states dissociated from taints but are not themselves taints; ...states that are taintless and dissociated from taints; ...states that are not objects of fetters; ...not objects of ties; ...not objects of floods; ...not objects of yokes; ...not objects of hindrances; ...states that are not misapprehended; ...states associated with misapprehension; ...states dissociated from misapprehension and not misapprehended; ...states that have an object: which phenomena are not included by the aggregate-count, not included by the sense-base-count, and not included by the element-count? With how many aggregates, sense bases, and elements are those phenomena associated? With none. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one sense base, and seven elements; from one sense base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

454. Anārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… no cittehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittavippayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā [P 0.0096]… cittavisaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasahabhūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… bāhirehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādādhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgena asaṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi [T 0.0110] katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttā? Te dhammā tīhi khandhehi sampayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici sampayuttā. Katihi vippayuttā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

454. Which phenomena, in relation to states without an object; in relation to states that are not mind; in relation to states dissociated from mind; in relation to states not mixed with mind; in relation to states that originate from mind; in relation to states co-nascent with mind; in relation to states consequent upon mind; in relation to external states; in relation to states that are clung to—which are not included by the aggregate-count, not included by the sense-base-count, not included by the element-count—with how many aggregates, sense bases, and elements are these phenomena associated? These phenomena are associated with three aggregates; with one sense base and one element, they are associated in some respects. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements; from one sense base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

455. Anupādāniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādānasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādānasampayuttehi ceva no ca upādānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādānavippayuttehi anupādāniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhehi ceva no ca kilesehi [M 0.0087][V 0.0089] dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesavippayuttehi asaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savitakkehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sappītikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… na kāmāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… rūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… arūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… apariyāpannehi dhammehi ye dhammā… niyyānikehi dhammehi ye dhammā [T 0.0111]… niyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anuttarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā khandhasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā āyatanasaṅgahena asaṅgahitā dhātusaṅgahena asaṅgahitā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi sampayuttāti? Natthi. Katihi [P 0.0097] vippayuttā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi vippayuttā; ekenāyatanena ekāya dhātuyā kehici vippayuttā.

455. Whatever phenomena are not objects of clinging; whatever phenomena are associated with clinging; whatever phenomena are associated with clinging but are not themselves clingings; whatever phenomena are not objects of clinging and are dissociated from clinging; whatever phenomena are non-defiling; whatever phenomena are undefiled; whatever phenomena are associated with defilements; whatever phenomena are defiled but are not themselves defilements; whatever phenomena are associated with defilements but are not themselves defilements; whatever phenomena are non-defiling and dissociated from defilements; whatever phenomena are to be abandoned by seeing; whatever phenomena are to be abandoned by development; whatever phenomena have causes to be abandoned by seeing; whatever phenomena have causes to be abandoned by development; whatever phenomena are with applied thought; whatever phenomena are with sustained thought; whatever phenomena are with rapture; whatever phenomena are accompanied by rapture; whatever phenomena are accompanied by happiness; whatever phenomena are accompanied by equanimity; whatever phenomena do not pertain to the sense-desire realm; whatever phenomena pertain to the form realm; whatever phenomena pertain to the formless realm; whatever phenomena are unincluded; whatever phenomena are emancipating; whatever phenomena are unsurpassed; whatever phenomena are with defilements—which are not included by the aggregate-count, not included by the sense-base-count, not included by the element-count—with how many aggregates, sense bases, and elements are these phenomena associated? None. With how many are they dissociated? They are dissociated from four aggregates, one sense base, and seven elements; from one sense base and one element, they are dissociated in some respects.

Rūpañca dhammāyatanaṃ dhammadhātu, itthipumaṃ jīvitaṃ nāmarūpaṃ;

Dve bhavā jātijarā maccurūpaṃ, anārammaṇaṃ no cittaṃ cittena vippayuttaṃ.

And form, the dhamma-base, the dhamma-element; femininity, masculinity, life faculty, name-and-form; the two kinds of becoming, birth, aging, death, and form; without an object, not mind, dissociated from mind.

Visaṃsaṭṭhaṃ samuṭṭhānasahabhu, anuparivatti bāhiraṃ upādā;

Dve visayo esanayo subuddhoti.

Not mixed, originating from and co-nascent, consequent upon, external, clung-to; the two sense fields, the method of seeking—thus it is well understood.

Asaṅgahitenasampayuttavippayuttapadaniddeso terasamo.

The thirteenth exposition of terms associated and dissociated in relation to the unincluded is finished.

14. Cuddasamanayo

14. The Fourteenth Method

14. Vippayuttenasaṅgahitāsaṅgahitapadaniddeso

14. Exposition of Terms Included and Unincluded in Relation to the Dissociated

1. Khandhādi

1. The Aggregates, etc.

456. Rūpakkhandhena [P 0.0098][M 0.0088][V 0.0090] ye dhammā vippayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

456. Phenomena that are dissociated from the form aggregate—by how many aggregates, by how many bases, and by how many elements are those phenomena included? Those phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they unincluded? They are unincluded by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

457. Vedanākkhandhena ye dhammā… saññākkhandhena ye dhammā… saṅkhārakkhandhena ye dhammā… viññāṇakkhandhena ye dhammā… manāyatanena ye dhammā… manindriyena ye dhammā vippayuttā, te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

457. Phenomena that are dissociated from the feeling aggregate... from the perception aggregate... from the formations aggregate... from the consciousness aggregate... from the mind base... from the mind faculty—by how many aggregates, by how many bases, and by how many elements are those phenomena included? Those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by one aggregate, eleven bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they unincluded? They are unincluded by four aggregates, one base, and seven elements.

458. Cakkhāyatanena [T 0.0112] ye dhammā…pe… phoṭṭhabbāyatanena ye dhammā… cakkhudhātuyā ye dhammā…pe… phoṭṭhabbadhātuyā ye dhammā vippayuttā…pe… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

458. Phenomena that are dissociated from the eye base... (and so on)... from the tangible base... from the eye element... (and so on)... from the tangible element... Those phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they unincluded? They are unincluded by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

459. Cakkhuviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… sotaviññāṇadhātuyā [P 0.0099] ye dhammā… ghānaviññāṇadhātuyā [V 0.0091] ye dhammā… jivhāviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… kāyaviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā… manodhātuyā ye dhammā… manoviññāṇadhātuyā ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi, ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

459. Phenomena that are dissociated from the eye-consciousness element... from the ear-consciousness element... from the nose-consciousness element... from the tongue-consciousness element... from the body-consciousness element... from the mind element... from the mind-consciousness element... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, they are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they unincluded? They are not unincluded by any aggregates, nor by any bases; they are unincluded by one element.

2. Saccādi

2. The Truths, etc.

460. Dukkhasaccena [M 0.0089] ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

460. Phenomena that are dissociated from the truth of suffering... Those phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they unincluded? They are unincluded by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

461. Samudayasaccena ye dhammā… maggasaccena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

461. Phenomena that are dissociated from the truth of origination... from the truth of the path... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, they are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not unincluded by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

462. Nirodhasaccena ye dhammā… cakkhundriyena ye dhammā [T 0.0113]… kāyindriyena ye dhammā… itthindriyena ye dhammā… purisindriyena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

462. Phenomena that are dissociated from the truth of cessation... from the eye faculty... from the body faculty... from the female faculty... from the male faculty... are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

463. Sukhindriyena ye dhammā… dukkhindriyena ye dhammā… somanassindriyena ye dhammā… domanassindriyena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ [P 0.0100] khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

463. Phenomena that are dissociated from the pleasure faculty... from the pain faculty... from the joy faculty... from the displeasure faculty... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not unincluded by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

464. Upekkhindriyena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi terasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

464. Phenomena that are dissociated from the equanimity faculty... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not unincluded by any aggregates or by any bases; they are not included by five elements.

465. Saddhindriyena [V 0.0092] ye dhammā… vīriyindriyena ye dhammā… satindriyena ye dhammā… samādhindriyena ye dhammā… paññindriyena ye [T 0.0114] dhammā… anaññātaññassāmītindriyena ye dhammā… aññindriyena ye dhammā… aññātāvindriyena ye dhammā… avijjāya ye dhammā… avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi [M 0.0090] dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

465. Phenomena that are dissociated from the faith faculty... from the energy faculty... from the mindfulness faculty... from the concentration faculty... from the wisdom faculty... from the faculty ‘I shall know the unknown’... from the final knowledge faculty... from the faculty of one who has final knowledge... from ignorance... from formations conditioned by ignorance... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not unincluded by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

466. Saṅkhārapaccayā viññāṇena ye dhammā… saḷāyatanapaccayā phassena ye dhammā… phassapaccayā vedanāya ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

466. Phenomena that are dissociated from consciousness conditioned by formations... from contact conditioned by the sixfold base... from feeling conditioned by contact... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by one aggregate, eleven bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, one base, and seven elements.

467. Vedanāpaccayā taṇhāya ye dhammā… taṇhāpaccayā upādānena ye dhammā… kammabhavena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

467. Phenomena that are dissociated from craving conditioned by feeling... from clinging conditioned by craving... from kamma-becoming... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not unincluded by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

468. Upapattibhavena ye dhammā… saññābhavena ye dhammā… pañcavokārabhavena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0101] asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

468. Phenomena that are dissociated from rebirth-becoming... from perceptual becoming... from five-constituent becoming... are included by four aggregates, two bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements.

469. Kāmabhavena [T 0.0115] ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi terasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

469. Phenomena that are dissociated from sensual-plane becoming... are included by four aggregates, two bases, and five elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and thirteen elements.

470. Rūpabhavena ye dhammā… asaññābhavena ye dhammā… ekavokārabhavena ye dhammā… paridevena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

470. Phenomena that are dissociated from fine-material-plane becoming... from non-percipient becoming... from one-constituent becoming... from lamentation... are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

471. Arūpabhavena [V 0.0093] ye dhammā… nevasaññānāsaññābhavena ye dhammā… catuvokārabhavena ye dhammā… sokena ye dhammā… dukkhena ye dhammā… domanassena ye dhammā… upāyāsena ye dhammā… satipaṭṭhānena ye dhammā… sammappadhānena ye dhammā… iddhipādena ye dhammā… jhānena ye dhammā… appamaññāya ye dhammā… pañcahi indriyehi ye dhammā… pañcahi balehi ye dhammā [M 0.0091]… sattahi bojjhaṅgehi ye dhammā… ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

471. Phenomena that are dissociated from immaterial-plane becoming... from neither-percipient-nor-non-percipient becoming... from four-constituent becoming... from sorrow... from pain... from grief... from despair... from the foundations of mindfulness... from the right strivings... from the bases for spiritual power... from jhāna... from the illimitables... from the five faculties... from the five powers... from the seven enlightenment factors... from the Noble Eightfold Path... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, they are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not unincluded by any aggregates, nor by any bases, nor by any elements.

3. Phassādisattakaṃ

3. The Septet Beginning with Contact

472. Phassena ye dhammā… vedanāya ye dhammā… saññāya ye dhammā… cetanāya ye dhammā… cittena ye dhammā… manasikārena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā ekena [T 0.0116] khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0102] asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

3. Phenomena that are dissociated from contact... from feeling... from perception... from volition... from consciousness... from attention... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, they are included by one aggregate, eleven bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by four aggregates, one base, and seven elements.

473. Adhimokkhena ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

473. Those phenomena that are dissociated from determination... setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, those phenomena are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate or any base, but are un-included by one element.

4. Tikaṃ

4. Triads

474. Kusalehi dhammehi ye dhammā… akusalehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

474. Those phenomena dissociated from wholesome states... those phenomena dissociated from unwholesome states... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena associated with pleasant feeling... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena associated with painful feeling... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate, any sense base, or any element.

475. Abyākatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi [V 0.0094] dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

475. Those phenomena dissociated from indeterminate phenomena... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

476. Adukkhamasukhāya [M 0.0092] vedanāya sampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… vipākehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ [P 0.0103] khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi terasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi [T 0.0117] pañcahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

476. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena associated with neither-painful-nor-pleasant feeling... those phenomena dissociated from resultant phenomena... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate or any sense base, but are un-included by five elements.

477. Vipākadhammadhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

477. Those phenomena dissociated from result-producing phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are defiled and objects of defilement... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate, any sense base, or any element.

478.
