Nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammehi ye dhammā… anupādinnupādāniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anupādinnaanupādāniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

478. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are neither resultant nor result-producing... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are not clung-to but are objects of clinging... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are not clung-to and are not objects of clinging... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are undefiled and are not objects of defilement... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements.

479. Upādinnupādāniyehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

479. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are clung-to and are objects of clinging... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and fifteen elements.

480. Asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

480. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are undefiled but are objects of defilement... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

481. Savitakkasavicārehi [T 0.0118] dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā [V 0.0095] pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā asaṅgahitā.

481. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena accompanied by applied thought and sustained thought... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and seventeen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate or any sense base, but are un-included by one element.

482. Avitakkavicāramattehi [M 0.0093] dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhasahagatehi dhammehi [P 0.0104] ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

482. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with only sustained thought, without applied thought... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena accompanied by joy... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena accompanied by pleasure... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate, any sense base, or any element.

483. Avitakkaavicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

483. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena without applied thought and sustained thought... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and fifteen elements.

484. Upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi terasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

484. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena accompanied by equanimity... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate or any sense base, but are un-included by five elements.

485. Dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ācayagāmīhi [T 0.0119] dhammehi ye dhammā… apacayagāmīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… sekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… mahaggatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

485. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena to be abandoned by vision… those phenomena dissociated from phenomena to be abandoned by development… those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with causes to be abandoned by vision… those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with causes to be abandoned by development… those phenomena dissociated from phenomena leading to accumulation… those phenomena dissociated from phenomena leading to decline… those phenomena dissociated from exalted phenomena… those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate, any sense base, or any element.

486. Neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi [P 0.0105] dhammehi ye dhammā… nevācayagāmināpacayagāmīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… nevasekkhanāsekkhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parittehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā [V 0.0096] catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

486. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena to be abandoned by neither vision nor development… those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with causes to be abandoned by neither vision nor development… those phenomena dissociated from phenomena neither leading to accumulation nor to decline… those phenomena dissociated from phenomena of neither trainees nor adepts… those phenomena dissociated from limited phenomena… those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

487. Appamāṇehi [M 0.0094] dhammehi ye dhammā… paṇītehi dhammehi ye vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

487. Those phenomena dissociated from immeasurable phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from sublime phenomena... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements.

488. Parittārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi dvādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na [T 0.0120] kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

488. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with limited objects... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any sense bases; they are not included by six elements.

489. Mahaggatārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… appamāṇārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hīnehi dhammehi ye dhammā… micchattaniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sammattaniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… maggādhipatīhi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

489. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with exalted objects... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with immeasurable objects... those phenomena dissociated from inferior phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena fixed in wrongness... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena fixed in rightness... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with the path as object... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with the path as cause... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with the path as predominance... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any sense bases, nor by any elements.

490. Majjhimehi dhammehi ye dhammā… aniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0106] asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

490. Those phenomena dissociated from intermediate phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from unfixed phenomena... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

491. Uppannehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anuppannehi dhammehi ye dhammā… uppādīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… atītehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anāgatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… paccuppannehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bahiddhāhi dhammehi ye dhammā… sanidassanasappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anidassanasappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi [T 0.0121] dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

491. Those phenomena dissociated from arisen phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from unarisen phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are bound to arise... those phenomena dissociated from past phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from future phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from present phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from internal phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from external phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are visible and impingent... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are invisible and non-impingent... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements.

492. Atītārammaṇehi [V 0.0097] dhammehi ye dhammā… anāgatārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bahiddhārammaṇehi dhammehi [M 0.0095] ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

492. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with past objects... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with future objects... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with internal objects... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with external objects... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any sense bases, nor by any elements.

493. Paccuppannārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi dvādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi chahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

493. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with present objects... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with internal and external objects... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any sense bases; they are not included by six elements.

5. Dukaṃ

5. The Couplet

494. Hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… sahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva sahetukehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… sahetukehi ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetūhi ceva hetusampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… hetusampayuttehi [P 0.0107] ceva na ca hetūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… na hetusahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi [T 0.0122] na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

494. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are roots... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with associated roots... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena associated with roots... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are both roots and with associated roots... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are with associated roots but are not roots... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are both roots and associated with roots... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are associated with roots but are not roots... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve sense bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregates, nor by any sense bases, nor by any elements.

495. Ahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… hetuvippayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… na hetuahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

495. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena without associated roots... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena dissociated from roots... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena that are not roots and are without associated roots... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and sixteen elements.

496. Appaccayehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṅkhatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sanidassanehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sappaṭighehi dhammehi ye dhammā… rūpīhi dhammehi ye dhammā… lokuttarehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [M 0.0096] asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

496. Those phenomena dissociated from phenomena without conditions... those phenomena dissociated from unconditioned phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from visible phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from phenomena with impingement... those phenomena dissociated from material phenomena... those phenomena dissociated from supramundane phenomena... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two sense bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten sense bases, and ten elements.

497. Lokiyehi [V 0.0098] dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

497. Which phenomena are dissociated from mundane phenomena... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

498. Āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva sāsavehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavehi ceva āsavasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavasampayuttehi ceva no ca āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ [P 0.0108] khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi [T 0.0123] na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

498. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are taints... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena associated with taints... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are both taints and objects of taints... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are both taints and associated with taints... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are associated with taints but are not taints... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not excluded by any aggregate, by any base, or by any element.

499. Sāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavavippayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sāsavehi ceva no ca āsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavavippayuttehi sāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

499. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are objects of taints... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena dissociated from taints... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are objects of taints but are not taints... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are dissociated from taints and are objects of taints... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

500. Anāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā… āsavavippayuttehi anāsavehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

500. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are not objects of taints... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are dissociated from taints and are not objects of taints... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

501. Saṃyojanehi dhammehi ye dhammā… ganthehi dhammehi ye dhammā… oghehi dhammehi ye dhammā… yogehi dhammehi ye dhammā… nīvaraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsehi ceva parāmaṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā[M 0.0097]? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

501. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are fetters... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are ties... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are floods... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are yokes... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are hindrances... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are graspings... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena associated with grasping... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are both graspings and objects of grasping... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not excluded by any aggregate, by any base, or by any element.

502. Parāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmaṭṭhehi ceva no ca parāmāsehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttehi parāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye [T 0.0124][V 0.0099] dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi [P 0.0109] khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

502. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are objects of grasping... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena dissociated from grasping... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are objects of grasping but are not graspings... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are dissociated from grasping and are objects of grasping... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

503. Aparāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… parāmāsavippayuttehi aparāmaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

503. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are not objects of grasping... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are dissociated from grasping and are not objects of grasping... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

504. Sārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cetasikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhūhi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattīhi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā ekena khandhena ekādasahāyatanehi ekādasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Catūhi khandhehi ekenāyatanena sattahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

504. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that have an object... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are consciousness... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are mental factors... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena associated with consciousness... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena conjoined with consciousness... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are both conjoined with and originated from consciousness... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are conjoined with, originated from, and co-existent with consciousness... which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that follow in sequence what is conjoined with and originated from consciousness... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in one aggregate, eleven bases, and eleven elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in four aggregates, one base, and seven elements.

505. Anārammaṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittavippayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… cittasaṃsaṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… upādādhammehi ye dhammā… anupādinnehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā[T 0.0125]? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

505. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena without an object... from phenomena dissociated from consciousness... from phenomena not conjoined with consciousness... from phenomena not clung-to... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

506. Upādinnehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi [P 0.0110] khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi tīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi pannarasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

506. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are clung-to... those phenomena are included in four aggregates, two bases, and three elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included in one aggregate, ten bases, and fifteen elements.

507. Upādānehi [M 0.0098] dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkilesikehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva saṃkiliṭṭhehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkiliṭṭhehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesehi ceva kilesasampayuttehi ca dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesasampayuttehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ [V 0.0100] khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

507. Which phenomena are dissociated from phenomena that are clingings... from phenomena that are defilements... from defiled phenomena... from phenomena associated with defilements... from phenomena that are both defilements and productive of defilements... from phenomena that are both defilements and defiled... from phenomena that are defiled but not defilements... from phenomena that are both defilements and associated with defilements... from phenomena that are associated with defilements but not defilements... those phenomena, setting aside the unconditioned from the count of aggregates, are included in five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not excluded by any aggregate, by any base, or by any element.

508. Saṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… asaṃkiliṭṭhehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesavippayuttehi dhammehi ye dhammā… saṃkilesikehi ceva no ca kilesehi dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesavippayuttehi saṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

508. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena that are bases for defilements... from undefiled phenomena... from phenomena dissociated from defilements... from phenomena that are bases for defilements but are not themselves defilements... from phenomena that are dissociated from defilements and are bases for defilements... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

509. Asaṃkilesikehi [T 0.0126] dhammehi ye dhammā… kilesavippayuttehi asaṃkilesikehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

509. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena that are not bases for defilements... from phenomena that are dissociated from defilements and are not bases for defilements... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

510. Dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… dassanena pahātabbahetukehi [P 0.0111] dhammehi ye dhammā… bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

510. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena to be abandoned by vision... from phenomena to be abandoned by cultivation... from phenomena with roots to be abandoned by vision... from phenomena with roots to be abandoned by cultivation... these phenomena, having set aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-category, are included by the five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate, any base, or any element.

511. Na dassanena pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… na bhāvanāya pahātabbehi dhammehi ye dhammā… na dassanena pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā… na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukehi dhammehi ye dhammā [M 0.0099] vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

511. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena not to be abandoned by vision... from phenomena not to be abandoned by cultivation... from phenomena with roots not to be abandoned by vision... from phenomena with roots not to be abandoned by cultivation... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

512. Savitakkehi dhammehi ye dhammā… savicārehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi sattarasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā[V 0.0101]. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi ekāya dhātuyā [T 0.0127] asaṅgahitā.

512. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena accompanied by applied thought... from phenomena accompanied by sustained thought... these phenomena, having set aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-category, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate or any base; they are un-included by one element.

513. Sappītikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pītisahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sukhasahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

513. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena accompanied by zest... from phenomena associated with zest... from phenomena associated with pleasure... these phenomena, having set aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-category, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate, any base, or any element.

514. Upekkhāsahagatehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi terasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0112] asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi pañcahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

514. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena associated with equanimity... these phenomena, having set aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-category, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and thirteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate or any base; they are un-included by five elements.

515. Kāmāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… pariyāpannehi dhammehi ye dhammā… sauttarehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

515. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena pertaining to the sensuous sphere... from phenomena included in the round of existence... from surpassable phenomena... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

516. Na kāmāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… apariyāpannehi dhammehi ye dhammā… anuttarehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi aṭṭhahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi dasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

516. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena not pertaining to the sensuous sphere... from phenomena not included in the round of existence... from unsurpassed phenomena... these phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and eight elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and ten elements.

517. Rūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… arūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… niyyānikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… niyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā [T 0.0128]… saraṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā… te dhammā asaṅkhataṃ khandhato ṭhapetvā [M 0.0100] pañcahi khandhehi dvādasahāyatanehi aṭṭhārasahi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi asaṅgahitā? Na kehici khandhehi na kehici āyatanehi na kāhici dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

517. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena of the fine-material sphere... from phenomena of the immaterial sphere... from phenomena that lead out... from fixed phenomena... from phenomena with defilements... these phenomena, having set aside the unconditioned from the aggregate-category, are included by five aggregates, twelve bases, and eighteen elements. By how many are they not included? They are not un-included by any aggregate, any base, or any element.

518. Na rūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… na arūpāvacarehi dhammehi ye dhammā… aniyyānikehi dhammehi ye dhammā… aniyatehi dhammehi ye dhammā… araṇehi dhammehi ye dhammā vippayuttā[V 0.0102], te dhammā katihi khandhehi katihāyatanehi katihi dhātūhi saṅgahitā? Te dhammā catūhi khandhehi dvīhāyatanehi dvīhi dhātūhi saṅgahitā. Katihi [P 0.0113] asaṅgahitā? Ekena khandhena dasahāyatanehi soḷasahi dhātūhi asaṅgahitā.

518. Those phenomena which are dissociated from phenomena not of the fine-material sphere... from phenomena not of the immaterial sphere... from phenomena that do not lead out... from unfixed phenomena... from phenomena without defilements... by how many aggregates, bases, and elements are these phenomena included? These phenomena are included by four aggregates, two bases, and two elements. By how many are they not included? They are not included by one aggregate, ten bases, and sixteen elements.

Dhammāyatanaṃ dhammadhātu, atha jīvitaṃ nāmarūpaṃ;

Saḷāyatanaṃ jātijarāmataṃ, dve ca tike na labbhare.

The mind-object base, the mind-object element, then life-faculty and name-and-form; the six bases, birth, aging, and death, and two terms from the triads are not found.

Paṭhamantare satta ca, gocchake dasa aparante;

Cuddasa cha ca matthake, iccete sattacattālīsa dhammā;

Samucchede na labbhanti, moghapucchakena cāti.

Seven in the first intermediate section, ten in the cluster, fourteen in the final section, and six at the head—thus these forty-seven phenomena are not found in the exposition of the fourteenth method, nor in the method of the empty question.

Vippayuttenasaṅgahitāsaṅgahitapadaniddeso cuddasamo.

The Fourteenth Method: The Exposition of Terms Included and Not Included in Relation to the Dissociated.

Dhātukathāpakaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.

The Treatise on the Discourse on Elements is concluded.
